    """Get current date."""

    time = datetime.datetime.now().time()
    WS_CURRENT_DATE_DATA_C1.ws_current_date = int(today.strftime("%Y%m%d"))

    WS_CURRENT_DATE_DATA_C1.ws_current_timestamp = today.strftime("%Y%m%d") + time.strftime("%H%M%S%f")[:8]

def process_deposits_C1() -> None:
    """Process deposits."""
    print("PROCESSING DEPOSITS...")
    WS_FLAGS_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while WS_FLAGS_C1.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ account_master NEXT
        #AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        #NOT AT END
        validate_deposit_C1()
        if WS_FLAGS_C1.ws_valid_flag == 'Y':
            post_deposit_C1()
            update_balance_C1()
            WS_COUNTERS_C1.ws_tran_count += 1
        WS_FLAGS_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    while WS_FLAGS_C1.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ loan_master NEXT
        #AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        #NOT AT END
        check_payment_status_C1()
        if WS_FLAGS_C1.ws_found_flag == 'N':
            mark_delinquent_C1()
            assess_late_fee_C1()
        WS_FLAGS_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def check_payment_status_C1() -> None:
    """Check payment status."""
    #IF loan_next_payment_date < ws_current_date
    #   SET ws_not_found TO TRUE
    WS_FLAGS_C1.ws_found_flag = 'Y'

def calculate_premiums_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates insurance premiums."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        insurance_record: InsuranceMasterC2 = InsuranceMasterC2(INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT=Decimal("0.00"), INS_CLAIMS_COUNT=0, INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT=Decimal("0.00")) # Dummy read for compilation
        if True: # Simulate NOT AT END condition
            determine_base_premium_C2(insurance_record)
            apply_risk_factor_C2(insurance_record)
            calculate_final_premium_C2(insurance_record)
        else:
            WS_EOF = True

def determine_base_premium_C2(insurance_record: InsuranceMasterC2) -> None:
    """Determines the base premium based on insurance type."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if INS_LIFE:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_record.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
    elif INS_HEALTH:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif INS_AUTO:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif INS_HOME:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_record.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
    elif INS_UMBRELLA:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_UMBRELLA_RATE

def apply_risk_factor_C2(insurance_record: InsuranceMasterC2) -> None:
    """Applies a risk factor to the calculated amount."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if insurance_record.INS_CLAIMS_COUNT > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal("1.25")

def calculate_final_premium_C2(insurance_record: InsuranceMasterC2) -> None:
    """Calculates the final premium and updates totals."""
    global WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS
    insurance_record.INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def calculate_portfolio_value_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates the portfolio value."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        investment_record: InvestmentMasterC2 = InvestmentMasterC2(INV_QUANTITY=0, INV_CURRENT_PRICE=Decimal("0.00"), INV_PURCHASE_PRICE=Decimal("0.00"), INV_DIVIDEND_RATE=Decimal("0.00"), INV_MARKET_VALUE=Decimal("0.00"), INV_GAIN_LOSS=Decimal("0.00")) # Dummy read for compilation
        if True: # Simulate NOT AT END condition
            calculate_position_value_C2(investment_record)
            calculate_gain_loss_C2(investment_record)
            update_totals_C2(investment_record)
        else:
            WS_EOF = True

def calculate_position_value_C2(investment_record: InvestmentMasterC2) -> None:
    """Calculates the position value of an investment."""
    investment_record.INV_MARKET_VALUE = investment_record.INV_QUANTITY * investment_record.INV_CURRENT_PRICE

def calculate_gain_loss_C2(investment_record: InvestmentMasterC2) -> None:
    """Calculates the gain or loss of an investment."""
    investment_record.INV_GAIN_LOSS = investment_record.INV_MARKET_VALUE - (investment_record.INV_QUANTITY * investment_record.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE)

def update_totals_C2(investment_record: InvestmentMasterC2) -> None:
    """Updates the total investment value."""
    global WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += investment_record.INV_MARKET_VALUE

def calculate_dividends_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates dividends."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        investment_record: InvestmentMasterC2 = InvestmentMasterC2(INV_QUANTITY=0, INV_CURRENT_PRICE=Decimal("0.00"), INV_PURCHASE_PRICE=Decimal("0.00"), INV_DIVIDEND_RATE=Decimal("0.00"), INV_MARKET_VALUE=Decimal("0.00"), INV_GAIN_LOSS=Decimal("0.00")) # Dummy read for compilation
        if True: # Simulate NOT AT END condition
            if investment_record.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE > 0:
                compute_dividend_C2(investment_record)
                post_dividend_C2()
        else:
            WS_EOF = True

def compute_dividend_C2(investment_record: InvestmentMasterC2) -> None:
    """Computes the dividend amount."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_CALC_AMOUNT = investment_record.INV_MARKET_VALUE * investment_record.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE / 4

def format_date_C2() -> None:
    """Formats a date."""

    """Validates an account."""
    global WS_VALID, WS_INVALID
    WS_VALID = True
    if ACCT_ID == " " * len(ACCT_ID):
        WS_INVALID = True

def calculate_tax_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates the tax amount."""
    global WS_CALC_TAX
    if WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
    else:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

def analyze_patterns_C2() -> None:
    """Analyzes transaction patterns for fraud."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("ANALYZING TRANSACTION PATTERNS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        transaction_record: TransactionRecordC2 = TransactionRecordC2("", "", Decimal("0.00"), "") # Dummy read for compilation
        if True: # Simulate NOT AT END condition
            check_amount_threshold_C2(transaction_record)
            check_frequency_C2()
            check_time_pattern_C2()
        else:
            WS_EOF = True

def check_amount_threshold_C2(transaction_record: TransactionRecordC2) -> None:
    """Checks if the transaction amount exceeds a threshold."""
    if transaction_record.TRAN_AMOUNT > 10000:
        flag_large_transaction_C2()

def flag_large_transaction_C2() -> None:
    """Flags a large transaction for audit."""
    global WS_PROCESS_COUNT
    WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
    write_audit_C2()

    pass

def behavioral_scoring_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates behavioral scores for customers."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("CALCULATING BEHAVIORAL SCORES...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        customer_record: CustomerMasterC2 = CustomerMasterC2(CUST_CREDIT_SCORE=0, CUST_TOTAL_LOANS=Decimal("0.00"), CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE=Decimal("0.00"), CUST_RISK_RATING="") # Dummy read for compilation
        if True: # Simulate NOT AT END condition
            calculate_risk_score_C2(customer_record)
            update_customer_profile_C2(customer_record)
        else:
            WS_EOF = True

def calculate_risk_score_C2(customer_record: CustomerMasterC2) -> None:
    """Calculates a risk score based on customer data."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = Decimal("0.00")
    if customer_record.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE < 600:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 30
    if customer_record.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS > customer_record.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 20

def update_customer_profile_C2(customer_record: CustomerMasterC2) -> None:
    """Updates the customer profile with the risk rating."""
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 50:
        customer_record.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'H'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 25:
        customer_record.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'M'
    else:
        customer_record.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'L'

def alert_generation_C2() -> None:
    """Generates fraud alerts based on analysis."""
    print("GENERATING FRAUD ALERTS...")

    pep_screening_C2()
    sanction_list_check_C2()

def aml_screening_C2() -> None:
    """Performs AML screening on transactions."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("PERFORMING AML SCREENING...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        transaction_record: TransactionRecordC2 = TransactionRecordC2("", "", Decimal("0.00"), "") # Dummy read for compilation
        if True: # Simulate NOT AT END condition
            if transaction_record.TRAN_AMOUNT >= 10000:
                ctr_filing_C2()
            structuring_check_C2()
        else:
            WS_EOF = True

def ctr_filing_C2() -> None:
    """Files a CTR (Currency Transaction Report)."""
    global WS_PROCESS_COUNT
    WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
    write_audit_C2()

def authorize_transaction_C2() -> None:
    """Authorizes a credit card transaction."""
    print("AUTHORIZING CREDIT CARD TRANSACTIONS...")
    check_credit_limit_C2()
    check_fraud_score_C2()
    send_authorization_C2()

def check_credit_limit_C2() -> None:
    """Checks if the transaction exceeds the credit limit."""
    global WS_NOT_APPROVED, WS_APPROVED
    account_master: AccountMasterC2 = AccountMasterC2(ACCT_OVERDRAFT_LIMIT=Decimal("0.00"), ACCT_BALANCE=Decimal("0.00")) # Dummy
    if WS_CALC_AMOUNT > account_master.ACCT_OVERDRAFT_LIMIT:
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True
    else:
        WS_APPROVED = True

def check_fraud_score_C2() -> None:
    """Checks the fraud score for the transaction."""
    pass

def send_authorization_C2() -> None:
    """Sends the authorization for the transaction."""
    if WS_APPROVED:
        write_transaction_C2()

def process_settlement_C2() -> None:
    """Processes credit card settlements."""
    print("PROCESSING CREDIT CARD SETTLEMENTS...")

def calculate_rewards_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates rewards points for credit card transactions."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_TOTAL_FEES
    transaction_record: TransactionRecordC2 = TransactionRecordC2("", "", Decimal("0.00"), "") # Dummy
    WS_CALC_RESULT = transaction_record.TRAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.01")
    WS_TOTAL_FEES += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_RESULT

def apply_interest_C2() -> None:
    """Applies interest to credit card balances."""
    global WS_CALC_INTEREST
    account_master: AccountMasterC2 = AccountMasterC2(ACCT_OVERDRAFT_LIMIT=Decimal("0.00"), ACCT_BALANCE=Decimal("0.00")) # Dummy
    WS_CALC_INTEREST = account_master.ACCT_BALANCE * WS_CREDIT_CARD_RATE / 12
    account_master.ACCT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_INTEREST

    print("GENERATING CREDIT CARD STATEMENTS...")

def dti_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates the Debt-to-Income (DTI) ratio."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_NOT_APPROVED
    customer_master: CustomerMasterC2 = CustomerMasterC2(CUST_CREDIT_SCORE=0, CUST_TOTAL_LOANS=Decimal("0.00"), CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE=Decimal("0.00"), CUST_RISK_RATING="") # Dummy
    loan_master: LoanMasterC2 = LoanMasterC2(LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT=Decimal("0.00"), LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE=Decimal("0.00"), LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE=Decimal("0.00"), LOAN_LTV_RATIO=Decimal("0.00")) # Dummy
    WS_CALC_RESULT = loan_master.LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT / (customer_master.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 12)
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > Decimal("0.43"):
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

def ltv_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates the Loan-to-Value (LTV) ratio."""
    global WS_CALC_FEE
    loan_master: LoanMasterC2 = LoanMasterC2(LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT=Decimal("0.00"), LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE=Decimal("0.00"), LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE=Decimal("0.00"), LOAN_LTV_RATIO=Decimal("0.00")) # Dummy
    loan_master.LOAN_LTV_RATIO = loan_master.LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE / loan_master.LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE
    if loan_master.LOAN_LTV_RATIO > Decimal("0.80"):
        WS_CALC_FEE += WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT

def credit_analysis_C2() -> None:
    """Performs credit analysis for mortgage applications."""
    global WS_NOT_APPROVED
    customer_master: CustomerMasterC2 = CustomerMasterC2(CUST_CREDIT_SCORE=0, CUST_TOTAL_LOANS=Decimal("0.00"), CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE=Decimal("0.00"), CUST_RISK_RATING="") # Dummy
    if customer_master.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE < 620:
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        try:
            global customer_master_index
            customer = customer_master[customer_master_index]
            customer_master_index += 1
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()
        except IndexError:
            ws_eof = True

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_calc_result
    ws_calc_result = (cust_total_balance * ws_savings_rate) + \
                     (cust_total_loans * ws_personal_rate) + \
                     (cust_total_investments * Decimal('0.01'))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_temp_code
    if ws_calc_result > 10000:
        ws_temp_code = 'PLATINUM'
    elif ws_calc_result > 5000:
        ws_temp_code = 'GOLD'
    elif ws_calc_result > 1000:
        ws_temp_code = 'SILVER'
    else:
        ws_temp_code = 'BRONZE'

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_calc_amount
    if acct_balance > acct_min_balance:
        ws_calc_amount = acct_balance - acct_min_balance
        global acct_balance, ws_total_investments
        acct_balance -= ws_calc_amount
        ws_total_investments += ws_calc_amount

def etl_processing_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    print("RUNNING ETL PROCESSES...")
    extract_data_C3()
    transform_data_C3()
    load_data_C3()

def extract_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof, ws_process_count, customer_master_index
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        try:
            customer = customer_master[customer_master_index]
            customer_master_index += 1
            ws_process_count += 1
        except IndexError:
            ws_eof = True

def transform_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    cleanse_data_C3()
    standardize_data_C3()
    enrich_data_C3()

def cleanse_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global cust_name, cust_last_name
    if cust_name == " ":
        cust_last_name = "UNKNOWN"

def standardize_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global cust_state
    cust_state = cust_state.upper()

def enrich_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    continue_C3()

def load_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    continue_C3()

def data_quality_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    print("CHECKING DATA QUALITY...")
    completeness_check_C3()
    accuracy_check_C3()
    consistency_check_C3()
    timeliness_check_C3()

def completeness_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_error_count, cust_id
    if cust_id == " ":
        ws_error_count += 1

def accuracy_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_error_count, cust_credit_score
    if cust_credit_score < 300 or cust_credit_score > 850:
        ws_error_count += 1

def consistency_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    continue_C3()

def timeliness_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_current_date, cust_last_activity, ws_error_count
    if cust_last_activity < ws_current_date - 365:
        ws_error_count += 1

@dataclass
class Customer:
    cust_id: str
    cust_name: str
    cust_last_name: str
    cust_state: str
    cust_credit_score: int
    cust_last_activity: int
    cust_total_balance: Decimal
    cust_total_loans: Decimal
    cust_total_investments: Decimal

ws_calc_result = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_deposits = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_withdrawals = Decimal("0.00")
ws_savings_rate = Decimal("0.00")
ws_personal_rate = Decimal("0.00")
loan_delinquent = False
cust_credit_score = 0
ws_error_count = 0
acct_balance = Decimal("0.00")
acct_min_balance = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_investments = Decimal("0.00")
ws_not_approved = False
ws_not_eof = False
ws_eof = False
ws_temp_code = ""
customer_master: List[Customer] = []
ws_process_count = 0
cust_name = ""
cust_last_name = ""
cust_state = ""
cust_id = ""

ws_current_date = 0

@dataclass
class DataRecordC4:
    """Data record."""
    cust_status: Optional[str] = None
    cust_ssn: Optional[str] = None
    cust_credit_score: Optional[int] = None
    cust_risk_rating: Optional[str] = None
    cust_total_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    cust_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
    tran_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    tran_log: Optional[str] = None
    loan_current_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    loan_paid_off: Optional[bool] = None
    ws_temp_code: Optional[str] = None
    ws_calc_result: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_deposits: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_calc_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_fees: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_not_eof: Optional[bool] = None
    ws_eof: Optional[bool] = None
    ws_process_count: Optional[int] = None
    ws_error_count: Optional[int] = None
    ws_valid: Optional[bool] = None
    ws_current_timestamp: Optional[str] = None
    ws_atm_fee_foreign: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_formatted_count: Optional[str] = None
    ws_cust_count: Optional[int] = None
    ws_temp_string: Optional[str] = None

def c100_transaction_monitoring_C4() -> None:
    """Transaction monitoring."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    global data_record_C4
    data_record_C4.ws_not_eof = True
    while not data_record_C4.ws_eof:
        # Simulate READ transaction_log NEXT
        # In a real implementation, you would read from a data source
        # For now, just set ws_eof to True after one iteration
        if not data_record_C4.ws_eof:  # Simulate NOT AT END
            c110_rule_based_detection_C4()
            c120_behavior_analysis_C4()
            c130_network_analysis_C4()
            data_record_C4.ws_eof = True  # Simulate AT END

def c110_rule_based_detection_C4() -> None:
    """Rule-based detection."""
    global data_record_C4
    if data_record_C4.tran_amount and data_record_C4.tran_amount >= 10000:
        c111_flag_ctr_C4()
    if data_record_C4.tran_amount and 5000 <= data_record_C4.tran_amount < 10000:
        c112_check_structuring_C4()

def c111_flag_ctr_C4() -> None:
    """Flag CTR."""
    global data_record_C4
    data_record_C4.ws_process_count = (data_record_C4.ws_process_count or 0) + 1

def d110_classification_C4() -> None:
    """Classification."""
    global data_record_C4
    if data_record_C4.cust_credit_score and data_record_C4.cust_credit_score > 750:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'A'
    elif data_record_C4.cust_credit_score and data_record_C4.cust_credit_score > 650:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'B'
    elif data_record_C4.cust_credit_score and data_record_C4.cust_credit_score > 550:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'C'
    else:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'D'

def d120_regression_C4() -> None:
    """Regression."""
    global data_record_C4
    data_record_C4.ws_calc_result = (data_record_C4.cust_credit_score or 0) * 10 + \
                                 ((data_record_C4.cust_total_balance or Decimal('0')) / 1000) - \
                                 ((data_record_C4.cust_total_loans or Decimal('0')) / 2000)

def main_loop_C5() -> None:
    """Main processing loop."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF_C5, WS_EOF_C5, WS_CUST_COUNT_C5
    WS_NOT_EOF_C5 = True
    while not WS_EOF_C5:
        try:
            # Assuming customer_master_next() reads the next customer record and returns it
            customer_record = customer_master_next()
            if customer_record is None:
                WS_EOF_C5 = True
            else:
                i110_update_profile_C5()
                i120_enrich_profile_C5()
                WS_CUST_COUNT_C5 += 1
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF_C5 = True

    """Simulates reading the next customer record."""
    pass

    """Main control paragraph."""

def initialization_1000_C5() -> None:
    """Initialize variables and files."""

    WS_CURR_YEAR_C5 = WS_CURRENT_DATETIME_C5[:4]
    WS_CURR_MONTH_C5 = WS_CURRENT_DATETIME_C5[4:6]

    RPT_MONTH_C5  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURR_MONTH_C5

def open_files_1100_C5() -> None:
    """Open input and output files."""
    global WS_FILE_STATUS_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5
    try:
        # Assuming appropriate file objects are created elsewhere
        customer_file = open("customer.dat", "r")
        account_file = open("account.dat", "r")
        transaction_file = open("transaction.dat", "r")
        report_file = open("report.txt", "w")
        error_file = open("error.log", "w")
        master_file = open("master.dat", "r+") # Read/Write

        WS_FILE_STATUS_C5 = "00"  # Simulate successful open

    except Exception as e:
        WS_FILE_STATUS_C5 = "99"  # Simulate file open error
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = "FILE OPEN ERROR"
        abort_process_9500_C5()
    finally:
        pass

def load_reference_data_1400_C5() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG_C5, WS_TRANS_COUNT_C5, WS_TRANSACTION_REC_C5
    try:

        if WS_TRANSACTION_REC_C5 is None:

            if WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 == 'Y':
                process_by_type_2200_C5()

                handle_error_2900_C5()
    except StopIteration:
        WS_EOF_FLAG_C5 = 'Y'

def validate_transaction_2100_C5() -> None:
    """Validate transaction record."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, TXN_TYPE_C5
    WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'Y'
    if TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5 == "" or TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5 is None:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    try:
        float(TXN_AMOUNT_C5) #IF txn_amount IS NOT NUMERIC
    except ValueError:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return
    if TXN_TYPE_C5 not in ('D', 'W', 'T', 'I'):
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'
    validate_account_exists_2150_C5()
    validate_business_rules_2160_C5()

def validate_account_exists_2150_C5() -> None:
    """Validate that the account exists."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY_C5, WS_VALID_FLAG_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5
    WS_SEARCH_KEY_C5  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG_C5 == 'N':
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def validate_business_rules_2160_C5() -> None:
    """Validate transaction against business rules."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5, TXN_TYPE_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5
    if TXN_TYPE_C5 == 'W':
        if TXN_AMOUNT_C5 > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5:
            WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
            WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if TXN_AMOUNT_C5 > Decimal("1000000.00"):
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'

def process_by_type_2200_C5() -> None:
    """Process transaction based on type."""
    global TXN_TYPE_C5
    if TXN_TYPE_C5 == 'D':
        process_deposit_2300_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE_C5 == 'W':
        process_withdrawal_2400_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE_C5 == 'T':
        process_transfer_2500_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE_C5 == 'I':
        process_interest_2600_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def process_deposit_2300_C5() -> None:
    """Process a deposit transaction."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, WS_TXN_DESC_C5, WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS_C5, WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT_C5
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5 += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT_C5
    WS_TXN_DESC_C5 = 'DEPOSIT'
    WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS_C5 += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT_C5
    WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT_C5 += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def update_account_2350_C5() -> None:
    """Update the account record in the master file."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5, ACCT_BALANCE_C5, WS_FILE_STATUS_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5
    ACCT_BALANCE_C5 = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5
    #ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = current_date()
    #rewrite account_record  # Rewrite the account record
    WS_FILE_STATUS_C5 = '00'  # Assume success
    if WS_FILE_STATUS_C5 != '00':
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'UPDATE FAILED'
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def write_audit_trail_2380_C5() -> None:
    """Write an audit trail record."""
    global TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, TXN_TYPE_C5, WS_JOB_ID_C5

    #audit_record.audit_job_id  = None  # TODO: was WS_JOB_ID
    #write_audit_record(audit_record)
    pass

def process_withdrawal_2400_C5() -> None:
    """Process a withdrawal transaction."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, WS_TXN_DESC_C5, WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS_C5, WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT_C5, WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT_C5
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5 -= None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT_C5
    WS_TXN_DESC_C5 = 'WITHDRAWAL'
    WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS_C5 += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT_C5
    WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT_C5 += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()
    if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5 < WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT_C5:
        generate_low_balance_alert_2450_C5()

def generate_low_balance_alert_2450_C5() -> None:

def process_transfer_2500_C5() -> None:
    """Process a transfer transaction."""
    validate_target_account_2510_C5()
    if WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 == 'Y':
        debit_source_2520_C5()
        credit_target_2530_C5()
        record_transfer_2540_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def validate_target_account_2510_C5() -> None:
    """Validate that the target account exists."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY_C5, TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT_C5, WS_VALID_FLAG_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5
    WS_SEARCH_KEY_C5 = TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT_C5
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG_C5 == 'N':
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def debit_source_2520_C5() -> None:
    """Debit the source account."""
    global WS_SOURCE_BALANCE_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, ACCT_BALANCE_C5
    WS_SOURCE_BALANCE_C5 -= None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT_C5
    ACCT_BALANCE_C5 = WS_SOURCE_BALANCE_C5
    #rewrite account_record

def credit_target_2530_C5() -> None:
    """Credit the target account."""
    global TXN_AMOUNT_C5, WS_TARGET_BALANCE_C5, ACCT_ID_C5, TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT_C5, ACCT_BALANCE_C5
    WS_TARGET_BALANCE_C5 += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT_C5
    ACCT_ID_C5 = TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT_C5
    #read master_file into WS_ACCOUNT_REC
    ACCT_BALANCE_C5 = WS_TARGET_BALANCE_C5
    #rewrite account_record
    pass

def record_transfer_2540_C5() -> None:
    """Record the transfer in audit trail."""
    global TXN_AMOUNT_C5, WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS_C5, WS_TRANSFER_COUNT_C5
    WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS_C5 += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT_C5
    WS_TRANSFER_COUNT_C5 += 1
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def process_interest_2600_C5() -> None:
    """Process an interest transaction."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5, WS_INTEREST_RATE_C5, WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT_C5, WS_TXN_DESC_C5, WS_TOTAL_INTEREST_C5, WS_INTEREST_COUNT_C5
    WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT_C5 = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5 * WS_INTEREST_RATE_C5 / 100
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5 += WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT_C5
    WS_TXN_DESC_C5 = 'INTEREST'
    WS_TOTAL_INTEREST_C5 += WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT_C5
    WS_INTEREST_COUNT_C5 += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def handle_error_2900_C5() -> None:
    """Handle an error condition."""
    global WS_ERROR_COUNT_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5, WS_MAX_ERRORS_C5, WS_ABORT_REASON_C5
    WS_ERROR_COUNT_C5 += 1
    #error_record = WSErrorRecord()
    #error_record.err_account  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    #error_record.err_message  = None  # TODO: was WS_ERROR_MSG
    #error_record.err_timestamp = current_date()
    #write_error_record(error_record)
    if WS_ERROR_COUNT_C5 > WS_MAX_ERRORS_C5:
        WS_ABORT_REASON_C5 = 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED'
        abort_process_9500_C5()
    pass

def batch_processing_3000_C5() -> None:
    """Process a batch of transactions."""
    load_batch_header_3100_C5()
    while WS_BATCH_EOF_C5 != 'Y':
        process_batch_items

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws_ltv_ratio: float, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Calculates the PMI amount based on LTV ratio."""
    if ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
    else:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount

def evaluate_history_C7(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int, ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:
    """Evaluates the history and adjusts risk score."""
    ws_factor_1 = ""
    ws_factor_2 = ""
    ws_factor_3 = ""
    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """Calculates final risk score and category."""
    ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score / 4
    ws_risk_category = ""
    if ws_risk_score >= 80:
        ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 60:
        ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 40:
        ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
    else:
        ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    return int(ws_risk_score), ws_risk_category

def determine_approval_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: float, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: float) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal]:
    """Determines loan approval status."""
    ws_approval_status = ""
    ws_conditions = ""
    ws_approved_amount = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_approved_rate = 0.0
    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, Decimal("0.00")
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, Decimal("0.00")
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, Decimal("0.00")
    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    ws_approved_rate = calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_credit_tier, ws_base_rate)
    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_base_rate: float) -> float:
    """Calculates approved loan terms."""
    ws_approved_rate = 0.0
    if ws_credit_tier == 'A':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + 0.00
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'B':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + 0.50
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'C':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + 1.50
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'D':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + 3.00
    return ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws_approved_rate: float, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[float, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Generates loan terms."""
    ws_loan_interest_rate = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    ws_loan_principal_bal = ws_loan_amount
    return ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, ws_loan_principal_bal

def create_amortization_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_monthly_rate: float, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool) -> tuple[list[Decimal], list[Decimal], list[Decimal], list[int], list[Decimal], list[Decimal], list[Decimal], int, int]:
    """Creates amortization schedule."""
    ws_running_balance = ws_loan_amount
    import datetime
    today = datetime.date.today()
    ws_payment_date = today
    amort_interest: list[Decimal] = []
    amort_principal: list[Decimal] = []
    amort_balance: list[Decimal] = []
    amort_payment_num: list[int] = []
    amort_payment_amt: list[Decimal] = []
    amort_escrow: list[Decimal] = []
    amort_total_pmt: list[Decimal] = []
    ws_payment_month: int = today.month
    ws_payment_year: int = today.year
    for ws_amort_idx in range(1, ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        amort_interest_value, amort_principal_value, ws_running_balance = calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance, ws_monthly_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, ws_property_tax, ws_insurance_premium, ws_pmi_amount, loan_mortgage, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year)
        amort_interest.append(amort_interest_value)
        amort_principal.append(amort_principal_value)
        amort_balance.append(ws_running_balance)
        amort_payment_num.append(ws_amort_idx)
        amort_payment_amt.append(ws_loan_monthly_pmt)
        if loan_mortgage:
            amort_escrow_value = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
            amort_escrow.append(amort_escrow_value)
            amort_total_pmt_value = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow_value + ws_pmi_amount
            amort_total_pmt.append(amort_total_pmt_value)
        else:
            amort_escrow.append(Decimal("0.00"))
            amort_total_pmt.append(ws_loan_monthly_pmt)
        ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year = advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year)
    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_escrow, amort_total_pmt, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance: Decimal, ws_monthly_rate: float, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool, ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates payment split."""
    amort_interest = ws_running_balance * Decimal(str(ws_monthly_rate))
    amort_principal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    ws_running_balance -= amort_principal
    return amort_interest, amort_principal, ws_running_balance

def advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Advances payment date."""
    ws_payment_month += 1
    if ws_payment_month > 12:
        ws_payment_month = 1
        ws_payment_year += 1
    return ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def finalize_loan_C7(ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_id: str, ws_loan_type: str, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_interest_rate: float, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal) -> None:
    """Finalizes loan process."""
    import datetime
    ws_loan_start_date = datetime.date.today()
    ws_loan_end_date = ws_loan_start_date + datetime.timedelta(days=(ws_loan_term_months * 30))
    ws_loan_status = 'A'
    create_loan_record_C7(ws_loan_id, ws_loan_type, ws_loan_amount, ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, ws_loan_start_date, ws_loan_status)
    disburse_funds_C7(ws_loan_amount)
    send_confirmation_C7()

def create_loan_record_C7(ws_loan_id: str, ws_loan_type: str, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_interest_rate: float, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, ws_loan_start_date: datetime.date, ws_loan_status: str) -> None:

    loan_rec_id = ws_loan_id
    loan_rec_type = ws_loan_type
    loan_rec_amount = ws_loan_amount
    loan_rec_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate
    loan_rec_payment = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    loan_rec_start = ws_loan_start_date
    loan_rec_status = ws_loan_status
    # WRITE loan_record FROM ws_loan_record
    pass

def load_portfolio_C7() -> tuple[List[Any], int]:
    """Loads portfolio data."""
    ws_hold_idx = 1
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    ws_holding: List[Any] = []
    while ws_hold_idx <= 100 and ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            # READ holdings_file INTO ws_holding_rec
            ws_holding_rec = None  # Replace with actual read operation
            if ws_holding_rec is None: # Simulate end of file
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                ws_holding.append(ws_holding_rec)
                ws_hold_idx += 1
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_holdings_count = ws_hold_idx - 1
    return ws_holding, ws_holdings_count

def update_market_prices_C7(ws_holdings_count: int, hold_symbol: List[str], hold_current_price: List[Decimal]) -> None:
    """Updates market prices for holdings."""
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        ws_quote_symbol = hold_symbol[ws_hold_idx - 1]
        ws_quote_price = get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol)
        hold_current_price[ws_hold_idx - 1] = ws_quote_price

def get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol: str) -> Decimal:
    """Gets quote for a symbol."""
    quote_request_symbol = ws_quote_symbol
    # CALL 'GETQUOTE' USING quote_request quote_response
    quote_response_status = 'OK'  # Replace with actual call and response
    quote_last_price = Decimal("100.00") # Set a default value. Replace with value from response
    if quote_response_status == 'OK':
        ws_quote_price = quote_last_price
    else:
        ws_quote_price = Decimal("0.00")
    return ws_quote_price

def calculate_values_C7(ws_holdings_count: int, hold_shares: List[int], hold_current_price: List[Decimal], hold_cost_per_share: List[Decimal], hold_market_value: List[Decimal], hold_gain_loss: List[Decimal], hold_pct_change: List[float]) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates values for holdings."""
    ws_total_value = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_cost_basis = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_unrealized_gain = Decimal("0.00")
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        hold_market_value[ws_hold_idx - 1], hold_gain_loss[ws_hold_idx - 1], hold_pct_change[ws_hold_idx - 1], ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain = calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_hold_idx, hold_shares, hold_current_price, hold_cost_per_share, hold_market_value, hold_gain_loss, hold_pct_change, ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain)
    return ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain

def calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_hold_idx: int, hold_shares: List[int], hold_current_price: List[Decimal], hold_cost_per_share: List[Decimal], hold_market_value: List[Decimal], hold_gain_loss: List[Decimal], hold_pct_change: List[float], ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_cost_basis: Decimal, ws_unrealized_gain: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, float, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates holding value."""
    hold_market_value_value = hold_shares[ws_hold_idx - 1] * hold_current_price[ws_hold_idx - 1]
    ws_hold_cost = hold_shares[ws_hold_idx - 1] * hold_cost_per_share[ws_hold_idx - 1]
    hold_gain_loss_value = hold_market_value_value - ws_hold_cost
    if ws_hold_cost > 0:
        hold_pct_change_value = (hold_gain_loss_value / ws_hold_cost) * 100
    else:
        hold_pct_change_value = 0.0
    ws_total_value += hold_market_value_value
    ws_cost_basis += ws_hold_cost
    ws_unrealized_gain += hold_gain_loss_value
    return hold_market_value_value, hold_gain_loss_value, hold_pct_change_value, ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain

def calc_auto_premium_C8(ws_driver_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_violations_3yr: int, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates auto premium."""
    if 6 <= ws_driver_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium += 100
    else:
        ws_base_premium += 50
    if ws_driver_age < 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_accidents_3yr > 0:
        ws_accident_surcharge = ws_accidents_3yr * 200
        ws_base_premium += ws_accident_surcharge
    if ws_violations_3yr > 0:
        ws_violation_surcharge = ws_violations_3yr * 100
        ws_base_premium += ws_violation_surcharge
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12
    pass

def calc_home_premium_C8(ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_home_age: int, ws_flood_zone: str, ws_security_system: str, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates home premium."""
    ws_base_premium = ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.003')
    if 0 <= ws_home_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    elif 11 <= ws_home_age <= 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 26 <= ws_home_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.2')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_flood_zone == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_security_system == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    ws_deductible_credit = ws_deductible / 1000 * 50
    ws_base_premium -= ws_deductible_credit
    if ws_base_premium < 200:
        ws_base_premium = Decimal('200')
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12
    pass

def calc_health_premium_C8(ws_insured_age: int, ws_plan_type: str, ws_family_plan: str, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates health premium."""
    ws_base_premium = Decimal('300')
    if 0 <= ws_insured_age <= 18:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.5')
    elif 19 <= ws_insured_age <= 30:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 31 <= ws_insured_age <= 40:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif 41 <= ws_insured_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    elif 51 <= ws_insured_age <= 60:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.0')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.8')
    if ws_plan_type == 'BRONZE':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.8')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'SILVER':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'GOLD':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'PLATINUM':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    if ws_family_plan == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.5')
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_annual_premium = ws_monthly_premium * 12
    pass

def underwriting_C8() -> None:
    """Performs underwriting."""
    evaluate_risk_factors_C8()
    check_medical_history_C8()
    verify_information_C8()
    determine_decision_C8()
    pass

def evaluate_risk_factors_C8(policy_life: bool, ws_bmi: int, ws_smoker_flag: str, ws_hazardous_occupation: str, policy_auto: bool, ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_risk_points: int) -> None:
    """Evaluates risk factors."""
    ws_risk_points = 0
    if policy_life:
        if ws_bmi > 30:
            ws_risk_points += 10
        if ws_smoker_flag == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 25
        if ws_hazardous_occupation == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 15
    if policy_auto:
        if ws_driver_age < 21:
            ws_risk_points += 20
        if ws_accidents_3yr > 1:
            ws_risk_points += 15
    pass

def check_medical_history_C8(ws_chronic_conditions: int, ws_recent_hospitalization: str, ws_prescription_count: int, ws_risk_points: int, ws_condition_points: int) -> None:
    """Checks medical history."""
    if ws_chronic_conditions > 0:
        ws_condition_points = ws_chronic_conditions * 5
        ws_risk_points += ws_condition_points
    if ws_recent_hospitalization == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10
    if ws_prescription_count > 5:
        ws_risk_points += 5
    pass

def verify_information_C8() -> None:
    """Verifies information."""
    check_fraud_indicators_C8()
    validate_documents_C8()
    pass

def check_fraud_indicators_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_address_mismatch: str, ws_risk_points: int, ws_fraud_flag: str) -> None:
    """Checks fraud indicators."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 3:
        ws_risk_points += 20
        ws_fraud_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_address_mismatch == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10
    pass

def validate_documents_C8(ws_doc_missing: str, ws_uw_status: str) -> None:
    """Validates documents."""
    if ws_doc_missing == 'Y':
        ws_uw_status = 'PENDING'
    else:
        ws_uw_status = 'COMPLETE'
    pass

def determine_decision_C8(ws_risk_points: int, ws_uw_decision: str, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Determines decision."""
    if ws_risk_points > 50:
        ws_uw_decision = 'DECLINE'
    elif ws_risk_points > 30:
        ws_uw_decision = 'SUBSTANDARD'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    elif ws_risk_points > 15:
        ws_uw_decision = 'STANDARD'
    else:
        ws_uw_decision = 'PREFERRED'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    pass

def issue_policy_C8(ws_uw_decision: str) -> None:
    """Issues policy."""
    if ws_uw_decision != 'DECLINE':
        generate_policy_number_C8()
        create_policy_record_C8()
        set_beneficiaries_C8()
        send_policy_docs_C8()
    else:
        send_decline_letter_C8()
    pass

def generate_policy_number_C8(ws_date_part: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_type_part: str, ws_random_part: int, ws_policy_number: str) -> None:
    """Generates policy number."""
    import datetime
    ws_date_part = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    ws_type_part = ws_policy_type
    import random
    ws_random_part = int(random.random() * 99999)
    ws_policy_number = f"{ws_type_part}{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}"
    pass

def create_policy_record_C8(ws_policy_number: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_effective_date: str, ws_expiration_date: str) -> None:
    """Creates policy record."""
    @dataclass
    class WsPolicyRecordC8:
        policy_rec_number: Optional[str] = None
        policy_rec_type: Optional[str] = None
        policy_rec_coverage: Optional[Decimal] = None
        policy_rec_premium: Optional[Decimal] = None
        policy_rec_eff_date: Optional[str] = None
        policy_rec_exp_date: Optional[str] = None
        policy_rec_status: Optional[str] = None
    ws_policy_record = WsPolicyRecordC8()
    policy_rec_number = ws_policy_number
    policy_rec_type = ws_policy_type
    policy_rec_coverage = ws_coverage_amount
    policy_rec_premium = ws_annual_premium
    policy_rec_eff_date = ws_effective_date
    policy_rec_exp_date = ws_expiration_date
    policy_rec_status = 'A'
    print("Writing Policy Record")
    print(f"Policy Number: {policy_rec_number}")
    print(f"Policy Type: {policy_rec_type}")
    print(f"Coverage Amount: {policy_rec_coverage}")
    print(f"Annual Premium: {policy_rec_premium}")
    print(f"Effective Date: {policy_rec_eff_date}")
    print(f"Expiration Date: {policy_rec_exp_date}")
    print(f"Status: {policy_rec_status}")
    pass

def set_beneficiaries_C8(ws_policy_number: str) -> None:
    """Sets beneficiaries."""
    benef_name = ["Beneficiary 1", "Beneficiary 2", "Beneficiary 3", "Beneficiary 4", "Beneficiary 5"]
    benef_relation = ["Spouse", "Child", "Parent", "Sibling", "Friend"]
    benef_pct = [50, 25, 10, 5, 10]

    @dataclass
    class WsBeneficiaryRecC8:
        benef_rec_policy: Optional[str] = None
        benef_rec_name: Optional[str] = None
        benef_rec_relation: Optional[str] = None
        benef_rec_pct: Optional[int] = None
    
    for ws_benef_idx in range(5):
        if benef_name[ws_benef_idx] != " ":
            ws_beneficiary_rec = WsBeneficiaryRecC8()
            benef_rec_policy = ws_policy_number
            benef_rec_name = benef_name[ws_benef_idx]
            benef_rec_relation = benef_relation[ws_benef_idx]
            benef_rec_pct = benef_pct[ws_benef_idx]
            print("Writing Beneficiary Record")
            print(f"Policy Number: {benef_rec_policy}")
            print(f"Name: {benef_rec_name}")
            print(f"Relation: {benef_rec_relation}")
            print(f"Percentage: {benef_rec_pct}")
    pass

def send_policy_docs_C8(ws_policy_number: str) -> None:
    """Sends policy documents."""
    ws_notif_type = 'policy_issue'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Your policy {ws_policy_number} has been issued'
    send_notification_C8()
    pass

def send_decline_letter_C8() -> None:
    """Sends decline letter."""
    ws_notif_type = 'policy_decline'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Regarding your insurance application'
    send_notification_C8()
    pass

def claims_handling_C8() -> None:
    """Handles claims."""
    receive_claim_C8()
    validate_claim_C8()
    investigate_claim_C8()
    adjudicate_claim_C8()
    process_payment_C8()
    pass

def receive_claim_C8(ws_claim_date: str, ws_claim_status: str) -> None:
    """Receives claim."""
    import datetime
    ws_claim_date = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    generate_claim_number_C8()
    ws_claim_status = 'RECEIVED'
    pass

def generate_claim_number_C8(ws_date_part: str, ws_random_part: int, ws_claim_number: str) -> None:
    """Generates claim number."""
    import datetime
    ws_date_part = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    import random
    ws_random_part = int(random.random() * 99999)
    ws_claim_number = f'CLM{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}'
    pass

def validate_claim_C8() -> None:
    """Validates claim."""
    check_policy_status_C8()
    check_coverage_C8()
    check_deductible_C8()
    pass

def check_policy_status_C8(ws_policy_status: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks policy status."""
    if ws_policy_status != 'A':
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'
    pass

def check_coverage_C8(ws_claim_type: str, ws_covered_perils: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks coverage."""
    if ws_claim_type != ws_covered_perils:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'NOT COVERED PERIL'
    pass

def check_deductible_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks deductible."""
    if ws_claim_amount <= ws_deductible:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE'
    pass

def investigate_claim_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_fraud_review: str, ws_adjuster_id: str, ws_notes: str) -> None:
    """Investigates claim."""
    if ws_claim_amount > 10000:
        ws_claim_status = 'INVESTIGATION'
        assign_adjuster_C8()
    fraud_check_C8()
    pass

def assign_adjuster_C8(ws_adjuster_id: str, ws_notes: str) -> None:
    """Assigns adjuster."""
    ws_adjuster_id = 'ADJ001'
    ws_notes = 'Assigned for investigation'
    pass

def fraud_check_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_fraud_review: str) -> None:
    """Checks for fraud."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 2:
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'
    if ws_claim_amount > ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.8'):
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'
    pass

def adjudicate_claim_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_approved_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Adjudicates claim."""
    if ws_claim_status != 'DENIED':
        ws_approved_amount = ws_claim_amount - ws_deductible
        if ws_approved_amount > ws_coverage_amount:
            ws_approved_amount = ws_coverage_amount
        ws_claim_status = 'APPROVED'
    pass

def process_payment_C8(ws_claim_status: str) -> None:
    """Processes payment."""
    if ws_claim_status == 'APPROVED':
        issue_payment_C8()
        update_claim_record_C8()
    pass

def issue_payment_C8(ws_claim_number: str, ws_approved_amount: Decimal, pay_rec_date: str, pay_rec_method: str) -> None:
    """Issues payment."""
    @dataclass
    class WsPaymentRecordC8:
        pay_rec_claim: Optional[str] = None
        pay_rec_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
        pay_rec_date: Optional[str] = None
        pay_rec_method: Optional[str] = None
    ws_payment_record = WsPaymentRecordC8()
    pay_rec_claim = ws_claim_number
    pay_rec_amount = ws_approved_amount
    import datetime
    pay_rec_date = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    pay_rec_method = 'CHECK'
    print("Writing Payment Record")
    print(f"Claim Number: {pay_rec_claim}")
    print(f"Amount: {pay_rec_amount}")
    print(f"Date: {pay_rec_date}")
    print(f"Method: {pay_rec_method}")
    pass

def update_claim_record_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_close_date: str) -> None:
    """Updates claim record."""
    ws_claim_status = 'PAID'
    import datetime
    ws_claim_close_date = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    print("Rewriting Claim Record")
    print(f"Claim Status: {ws_claim_status}")
    print(f"Claim Close Date: {ws_claim_close_date}")
    pass

def payroll_processing_C8() -> None:
    """Processes payroll."""
    load_employee_data_C8()
    calculate_gross_pay_C8()
    calculate_taxes_C8()
    calculate_deductions_C8()
    calculate_net_pay_C8()
    generate_paystubs_C8()
    process_direct_deposit_C8()
    pass

def load_employee_data_C8(ws_employee_id: str, emp_search_key: str, ws_error_msg: str) -> None:
    """Loads employee data."""
    emp_search_key = ws_employee_id
    try:
        ws_employee_rec = {}
    except:
        ws_error_msg = 'EMPLOYEE NOT FOUND'
        handle_error_C8()
    pass

def calculate_gross_pay_C8(ws_pay_type: str) -> None:
    """Calculates gross pay."""
    if ws_pay_type == 'SALARY':
        calc_salary_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'HOURLY':
        calc_hourly_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'COMMISSION':
        calc_commission_pay_C8()
    pass

def calc_salary_pay_C8(ws_annual_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates salary pay."""
    ws_gross_pay = ws_annual_salary / ws_pay_periods
    pass

def calc_hourly_pay_C8(ws_hours_worked: Decimal, ws_hourly_rate: Decimal, ws_regular_pay: Decimal, ws_overtime_pay: Decimal, ws_ot_hours: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates hourly pay."""
    if ws_hours_worked <= 40:
        ws_regular_pay = ws_hours_worked * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_overtime_pay = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_regular_pay = 40 * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_ot_hours = ws_hours_worked - 40
        ws_overtime_pay = ws_ot_hours * ws_hourly_rate * Decimal('1.5')
    ws_gross_pay = ws_regular_pay + ws_overtime_pay
    pass

def calc_commission_pay_C8(ws_base_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_sales_amount: Decimal, ws_commission_rate: Decimal, ws_base_pay: Decimal, ws_commission_pay: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates commission pay."""
    ws_base_pay = ws_base_salary / ws_pay_periods
    ws_commission_pay = ws_sales_amount * ws_commission_rate
    ws_gross_pay = ws_base_pay + ws_commission_pay
    pass

def calculate_taxes_C8() -> None:
    """Calculates taxes."""
    calc_federal_tax_C8()
    calc_state_tax_C8()
    calc_local_tax_C8()
    calc_fica_C8()
    pass

def calc_federal_tax_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_exemptions: int, ws_annualized_gross: Decimal, ws_allowance_amount: Decimal, ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates federal tax."""
    ws_annualized_gross = ws_gross_pay * ws_pay_periods
    ws_allowance_amount = ws_exemptions * 4300
    ws_taxable_income = ws_annualized_gross - ws_allowance_amount
    if ws_taxable_income < 0:
        ws_taxable_income = Decimal('0')
    apply_tax_brackets_C8()
    ws_federal_tax = ws_annual_tax / ws_pay_periods
    pass

def apply_tax_brackets_C8(status_single: bool, status_married_joint: bool, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Applies tax brackets."""
    ws_annual_tax = Decimal('0')
    if status_single:
        single_brackets_C8()
    elif status_married_joint:
        married_brackets_C8()
    pass

def single_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates single brackets."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 10275:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal('0.10')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 41775:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('1027.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 10275) * Decimal('0.12')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 89075:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('4807.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 41775) * Decimal('0.22')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 170050:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('15213.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 89075) * Decimal('0.24')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 215950:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('34647.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 170050) * Decimal('0.32')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 539900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('49335.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 215950) * Decimal('0.35')
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('162718.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 539900) * Decimal('0.37')
    pass

def married_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates married brackets."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 20550:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal('0.10')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 83550:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('2055.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 20550) * Decimal('0.12')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 178150:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('9615.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 83550) * Decimal('0.22')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 340100:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('30427.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 178150) * Decimal('0.24')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 431900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('69295.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 340100) * Decimal('0.32')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 647850:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('98671.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 431900) * Decimal('0.35')
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('174253.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 647850) * Decimal('0.37')
    pass

def calc_state_tax_C8(ws_state_code: str, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates state tax."""
    if ws_state_code == 'CA':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0725')
    elif ws_state_code == 'NY':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0685')
    elif ws_state_code == 'TX':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal('0')
    elif ws_state_code == 'FL':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.05')
    pass

def calc_local_tax_C8(ws_local_tax_rate: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates local tax."""
    if ws_local_tax_rate > 0:
        ws_local_tax = ws_gross_pay * ws_local_tax_rate
    else:
        ws_local_tax = Decimal('0')
    pass

def calc_fica_C8(ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_remaining_cap: Decimal, ws_additional_medicare: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates FICA."""
    if ws_ytd_gross < 160200:
        ws_remaining_cap = 160200 - ws_ytd_gross
        if ws_gross_pay <= ws_remaining_cap:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.062')
        else:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_remaining_cap * Decimal('0.062')
    else:
        ws_fica_ss = Decimal('0')
    ws_fica_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0145')
    if ws_ytd_gross > 200000:
        ws_additional_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.009')
        ws_fica_medicare += ws_additional_medicare
    pass

def calculate_deductions_C8() -> None:
    """Calculates deductions."""
    calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8()
    calc_post_tax_deductions_C8()
    pass

def calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8(ws_401k_pct: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_ytd_401k: Decimal, ws_health_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_hsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates pre-tax deductions."""
    if ws_401k_pct > 0:
        ws_401k_contrib = ws_gross_pay * ws_401k_pct / 100
        if ws_ytd_401k + ws_401k_contrib > 22500:
            ws_401k_contrib = 22500 - ws_ytd_401k
            if ws_401k_contrib < 0:
                ws_401k_contrib = Decimal('0')
    ws_health_ins = ws_health_ins_deduct
    ws_dental_ins = ws_dental_ins_deduct
    ws_vision_ins = ws_vision_ins_deduct
    ws_hsa_contrib = ws_hsa_deduct
    ws_fsa_contrib = ws_fsa_deduct
    pass

def calc_post_tax_deductions_C8(ws_life_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_deduct: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues_amt: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishment_amt: Decimal, ws_garnishment: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates post-tax deductions."""
    ws_life_ins = ws_life_ins_deduct
    ws_disability_ins = ws_disability_deduct
    ws_union_dues = ws_union_dues_amt
    ws_garnishment = ws_garnishment_amt
    pass

def calculate_net_pay_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishment: Decimal, ws_other_deduct: Decimal, ws_total_deductions: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates net pay."""
    ws_total_deductions = (ws_federal_tax + ws_state_tax + ws_local_tax + ws_fica_ss + ws_fica_medicare + ws_health_ins + ws_dental_ins + ws_vision_ins + ws_401k_contrib + ws_hsa_contrib + ws_fsa_contrib + ws_life_ins + ws_disability_ins + ws_union_dues + ws_garnishment + ws_other_deduct)
    ws_net_pay = ws_gross_pay - ws_total_deductions
    update_ytd_totals_C8()
    pass

def update_ytd_totals_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_ytd_fed_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_ytd_state_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws

def check_adverse_media_C9() -> None:
    """Check adverse media."""
    media_search_name: str
    media_request: Any
    media_response: Any
    media_hits_found: int
    ws_watchlist_hits: int
    media_search_name = ws_customer_name_C9
    # CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING media_request media_response
    if media_hits_found > 0:
        ws_watchlist_hits += media_hits_found

def calculate_match_score_C9() -> None:
    """Calculate match score."""
    ws_ofac_score: int
    ws_pep_score: int
    ws_match_score: int
    ws_watchlist_hits: int
    if ws_ofac_score > 0:
        ws_match_score += ws_ofac_score
    if ws_pep_score > 0:
        ws_match_score += ws_pep_score
    ws_match_score = (ws_match_score / ws_watchlist_hits)

def determine_disposition_C9() -> None:
    """Determine disposition."""
    ws_match_score: int
    ws_match_type: str
    ws_sar_required: str
    ws_case_status: str
    if ws_match_score >= 90:
        ws_match_type = 'CONFIRMED'
        ws_sar_required = 'Y'
    elif ws_match_score >= 75:
        ws_match_type = 'POTENTIAL'
        ws_case_status = 'REVIEW'
    elif ws_match_score >= 50:
        ws_match_type = 'WEAK'
        ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
    else:
        ws_match_type = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
        ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'

    ws_manual_review: str

        ws_manual_review = 'Y'
    elif ws_fraud_score >= 60:

        ws_manual_review = 'Y'

    else:

def categorize_case_C9() -> None:
    """Categorize case."""
    ws_case_type: str

    ws_open_date_C9: Any
    ws_target_date: int

    elif ws_case_type == 'FRAUD REPORT':

    elif ws_case_type == 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':

    elif ws_case_type == 'GENERAL INQUIRY':
        ws_case_priority = 3
    else:
        ws_case_priority = 3
    ws_target_date = integer_of_date_C9(ws_open_date_C9) + (ws_case_priority * 2)  # Assuming integer_of_date is a function

def execute_steps_C9() -> None:
    """Execute steps."""
    ws_current_step: int
    ws_total_steps: int
    ws_workflow_status: str
    while not (ws_current_step > ws_total_steps or ws_workflow_status == 'FAILED'):
        execute_current_step_C9()
        ws_current_step += 1

def execute_current_step_C9() -> None:
    """Execute current step."""
    ws_current_step: int
    step_start_date: list[Any] = []
    step_status: list[str] = []
    step_name: list[str] = []
    step_end_date: list[Any] = []
    step_outcome: list[str] = []
    step_start_date[ws_current_step] = current_date_C9()  # Assuming current_date is a function
    step_status[ws_current_step] = 'in_progress'
    if step_name[ws_current_step] == 'VALIDATION':
        validation_step_C9()
    elif step_name[ws_current_step] == 'APPROVAL':
        approval_step_C9()
    elif step_name[ws_current_step] == 'PROCESSING':
        processing_step_C9()
    elif step_name[ws_current_step] == 'NOTIFICATION':
        notification_step_C9()
    else:
        generic_step_C9()
    step_end_date[ws_current_step] = current_date_C9()  # Assuming current_date is a function

def validation_step_C9() -> None:
    """Validation step."""
    ws_validation_passed: str
    ws_current_step: int
    step_status: list[str] = []
    step_outcome: list[str] = []
    ws_workflow_status: str
    if ws_validation_passed == 'Y':
        step_status[ws_current_step] = 'COMPLETED'
        step_outcome[ws_current_step] = 'VALIDATED'
    else:
        step_status[ws_current_step] = 'FAILED'
        step_outcome[ws_current_step] = 'VALIDATION FAILED'
        ws_workflow_status = 'FAILED'

def approval_step_C9() -> None:
    """Approval step."""
    ws_approval_received: str
    ws_rejection_received: str
    ws_current_step: int
    step_status: list[str] = []
    step_outcome: list[str] = []
    ws_workflow_status: str
    if ws_approval_received == 'Y':
        step_status[ws_current_step] = 'COMPLETED'
        step_outcome[ws_current_step] = 'APPROVED'
    elif ws_rejection_received == 'Y':
        step_status[ws_current_step] = 'COMPLETED'
        step_outcome[ws_current_step] = 'REJECTED'
        ws_workflow_status = 'FAILED'
    else:
        step_status[ws_current_step] = 'PENDING'
        ws_current_step -= 1

def processing_step_C9() -> None:
    """Processing step."""
    ws_current_step: int
    step_status: list[str] = []
    step_outcome: list[str] = []
    step_status[ws_current_step] = 'COMPLETED'
    step_outcome[ws_current_step] = 'PROCESSED'

def notification_step_C9() -> None:
    """Notification step."""
    ws_current_step: int
    step_status: list[str] = []
    step_outcome: list[str] = []
    send_notification_C9()
    step_status[ws_current_step] = 'COMPLETED'
    step_outcome[ws_current_step] = 'NOTIFIED'

def generic_step_C9() -> None:
    """Generic step."""
    ws_current_step: int
    step_status: list[str] = []
    step_outcome: list[str] = []
    step_status[ws_current_step] = ''

import datetime


def calculate_next_run_date_C10(ws_last_run_date: datetime.date, frequency: str) -> datetime.date:
    """Calculates the next run date based on frequency."""
    integer_date = ws_last_run_date.toordinal()
    if frequency == 'WEEKLY':
        ws_next_run_date = datetime.date.fromordinal(integer_date + 7)
    elif frequency == 'MONTHLY':
        ws_next_run_date = datetime.date.fromordinal(integer_date + 30)
    elif frequency == 'QUARTERLY':
        ws_next_run_date = datetime.date.fromordinal(integer_date + 90)
    elif frequency == 'YEARLY':
        ws_next_run_date = datetime.date.fromordinal(integer_date + 365)
    else:
        ws_next_run_date = datetime.date.fromordinal(integer_date + 1)
    return ws_next_run_date

    calculate_kpi_C10()

def collect_metrics_C10():
    """Collects various metrics."""
    collect_transaction_metrics_C10()
    collect_customer_metrics_C10()
    collect_performance_metrics_C10()

def collect_transaction_metrics_C10():
    """Collects transaction metrics."""
    ws_total_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_total_trans_count: int = 0
    ws_avg_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_trans_rec = read_transaction_file()
            ws_total_trans_count += 1
            ws_total_trans_amount += ws_trans_rec.trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_avg_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount / ws_total_trans_count

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def read_transaction_file():
    """Reads a transaction record from the transaction file."""
    pass

def collect_customer_metrics_C10():
    """Collects customer metrics."""
    ws_active_customers: int = 0
    ws_new_customers: int = 0
    ws_churned_customers: int = 0
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_period_start: datetime.date = datetime.date(2024, 1, 1)

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec = read_customer_file()
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_status == 'A':
                ws_active_customers += 1
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_open_date >= ws_period_start:
                ws_new_customers += 1
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_close_date >= ws_period_start:
                ws_churned_customers += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def read_customer_file():
    """Reads a customer record from the customer file."""
    pass

def collect_performance_metrics_C10():
    """Collects performance metrics."""
    ws_response_time_total: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_response_count: int = 0
    ws_avg_response_time: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_perf_rec = read_perf_log_file()
            ws_response_time_total += ws_perf_rec.perf_response_time
            ws_response_count += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    if ws_response_count > 0:
        ws_avg_response_time = ws_response_time_total / ws_response_count

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    """Reads a performance log record from the performance log file."""
    pass

    ws_total_trans_count: int = 100
    ws_total_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal("1000.00")
    ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("500.00")
    ws_total_withdrawals: Decimal = Decimal("500.00")

    ws_daily_summary.daily_date = ws_process_date
    ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count = ws_total_trans_count
    ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount
    ws_daily_summary.daily_deposits = ws_total_deposits
    ws_daily_summary.daily_withdrawals = ws_total_withdrawals
    write_daily_summary_record(ws_daily_summary)

def weekly_aggregation_C10():
    """Performs weekly data aggregation."""

    if ws_day_of_week == 7:
        ws_weekly_summary = WeeklySummaryC10()
        ws_weekly_summary.weekly_week = ws_week_number
        sum_week_data_C10()
        write_weekly_summary_record(ws_weekly_summary)

def sum_week_data_C10():
    """Sums weekly data from daily summaries."""
    weekly_trans_count: int = 0
    weekly_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    for _ in range(7):
        daily_trans_count: int = 10
        daily_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal("100.00")
        weekly_trans_count += daily_trans_count
        weekly_trans_amount += daily_trans_amount

def monthly_aggregation_C10():
    """Performs monthly data aggregation."""
    ws_end_of_month: str = 'Y'
    ws_curr_month: int = 12
    ws_curr_year: int = 2024

    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        ws_monthly_summary = MonthlySummaryC10()
        ws_monthly_summary.monthly_month = ws_curr_month
        ws_monthly_summary.monthly_year = ws_curr_year
        sum_month_data_C10()
        write_monthly_summary_record(ws_monthly_summary)

def sum_month_data_C10():
    """Sums monthly data from daily summaries."""
    monthly_trans_count: int = 0
    monthly_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    monthly_new_accounts: int = 0
    monthly_closed_accounts: int = 0
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_curr_month: int = 12

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file()
            if ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date.month == ws_curr_month:
                monthly_trans_count += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count
                monthly_trans_amount += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    """Reads a daily summary record from the daily summary file."""
    pass

def calculate_kpi_C10():
    """Calculates key performance indicators."""
    calc_financial_kpi_C10()
    calc_operational_kpi_C10()
    calc_customer_kpi_C10()

def calc_financial_kpi_C10():
    """Calculates financial KPIs."""
    ws_total_assets: Decimal = Decimal("1000000.00")
    ws_net_income: Decimal = Decimal("50000.00")
    ws_total_equity: Decimal = Decimal("600000.00")
    ws_interest_expense: Decimal = Decimal("10000.00")
    ws_interest_income: Decimal = Decimal("20000.00")
    ws_earning_assets: Decimal = Decimal("800000.00")
    ws_roa: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_roe: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_nim: Decimal = Decimal("0")

    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_roa = (ws_net_income / ws_total_assets) * 100

    if ws_total_equity > 0:
        ws_roe = (ws_net_income / ws_total_equity) * 100

    if ws_interest_expense > 0:
        ws_nim = ((ws_interest_income - ws_interest_expense) / ws_earning_assets) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi_C10():
    """Calculates operational KPIs."""
    ws_total_trans_count: int = 1000
    ws_error_count: int = 10
    ws_within_sla_count: int = 950
    ws_total_cases: int = 1000
    ws_fcr_count: int = 800
    ws_total_calls: int = 1000
    ws_error_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_sla_compliance: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_first_call_resolution: Decimal = Decimal("0")

    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_error_rate = (ws_error_count / ws_total_trans_count) * 100

    ws_sla_compliance = (ws_within_sla_count / ws_total_cases) * 100
    ws_first_call_resolution = (ws_fcr_count / ws_total_calls) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi_C10():
    """Calculates customer KPIs."""
    ws_active_customers: int = 1000
    ws_churned_customers: int = 50
    ws_marketing_spend: Decimal = Decimal("10000.00")
    ws_new_customers: int = 100
    ws_avg_revenue_per_customer: Decimal = Decimal("500.00")
    ws_avg_customer_tenure: int = 3
    ws_churn_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_acquisition_cost: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_lifetime_value: Decimal = Decimal("0")

    if ws_active_customers > 0:
        ws_churn_rate = (ws_churned_customers / ws_active_customers) * 100

    ws_acquisition_cost = ws_marketing_spend / ws_new_customers
    ws_lifetime_value = ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * ws_avg_customer_tenure

def export_data_C10():
    """Exports data to various formats."""
    export_csv_C10()
