def export_csv_C10():
    """Exports data to CSV format."""
    csv_export_file = open("daily_summary.csv", "w")
    ws_csv_header: str = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
    write_csv_record(csv_export_file, ws_csv_header)
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file()
            daily_date: str = str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date)
            daily_trans_count: str = str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count)
            daily_trans_amount: str = str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount)
            daily_deposits: str = str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_deposits)
            daily_withdrawals: str = str(ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_withdrawals)

            ws_csv_line: str = (
                f"{daily_date},{daily_trans_count},{daily_trans_amount},{daily_deposits},{daily_withdrawals}"
            )
            write_csv_record(csv_export_file, ws_csv_line)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    csv_export_file.close()
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def write_csv_record(csv_export_file, csv_line):
    """Writes a CSV record to the CSV file."""
    csv_export_file.write(csv_line + '\n')

def export_xml_C10():
    """Exports data to XML format."""
    xml_export_file = open("daily_summary.xml", "w")
    ws_xml_line: str = '<?xml version="1.0"?>'
    write_xml_record(xml_export_file, ws_xml_line)
    ws_xml_line: str = '<DailySummaries>'
    write_xml_record(xml_export_file, ws_xml_line)
    write_xml_records_C10(xml_export_file)
    ws_xml_line: str = '</DailySummaries>'
    write_xml_record(xml_export_file, ws_xml_line)
    xml_export_file.close()

def write_xml_record(xml_export_file, xml_line):
    """Writes an XML record to the XML file."""
    xml_export_file.write(xml_line + '\n')

def write_xml_records_C10(xml_export_file):
    """Writes XML records from daily summaries."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file()
            format_xml_record_C10(xml_export_file, ws_daily_sum_rec)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_xml_record_C10(xml_export_file, ws_daily_sum_rec):
    """Formats an XML record from a daily summary."""
    ws_xml_line: str = '<Summary>'
    write_xml_record(xml_export_file, ws_xml_line)
    ws_xml_line: str = f'<Date>{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date}</Date>'
    write_xml_record(xml_export_file, ws_xml_line)
    ws_xml_line: str = f'<TransCount>{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count}</TransCount>'
    write_xml_record(xml_export_file, ws_xml_line)
    ws_xml_line: str = '</Summary>'
    write_xml_record(xml_export_file, ws_xml_line)

def export_json_C10():
    """Exports data to JSON format."""
    json_export_file = open("daily_summary.json", "w")
    ws_json_line: str = '{"dailySummaries":['
    write_json_record(json_export_file, ws_json_line)
    write_json_records_C10(json_export_file)
    ws_json_line: str = ']}'
    write_json_record(json_export_file, ws_json_line)
    json_export_file.close()

def write_json_record(json_export_file, json_line):
    """Writes a JSON record to the JSON file."""
    json_export_file.write(json_line + '\n')

def write_json_records_C10(json_export_file):
    """Writes JSON records from daily summaries."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_first_record: str = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file()
            format_json_record_C10(json_export_file, ws_daily_sum_rec, ws_first_record)
            ws_first_record = 'Y'
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_json_record_C10(json_export_file, ws_daily_sum_rec, ws_first_record):
    """Formats a JSON record from a daily summary."""
    if ws_first_record == 'Y':
        ws_json_comma: str = ','
    else:
        ws_json_comma: str = ''
        ws_first_record = 'Y'

    ws_json_line: str = (
        f'{ws_json_comma}{{"date":"{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date}",'
        f'"transCount":{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count},'
        f'"transAmount":{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount}}}'
    )
    write_json_record(json_export_file, ws_json_line)

def account_maintenance_C10():
    """Performs account maintenance procedures."""
    dormant_account_check_C10()
    escheatment_processing_C10()
    account_closure_C10()
    account_reactivation_C10()

def dormant_account_check_C10():
    """Checks for dormant accounts."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = read_account_file()
            check_activity_C10(ws_account_rec)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def read_account_file():
    """Reads an account record from the account file."""
    pass

def check_activity_C10(ws_account_rec):
    """Checks account activity and marks dormant if necessary."""
    ws_process_date: datetime.date = datetime.date(2024, 1, 1)
    ws_days_inactive: int = (ws_process_date.toordinal() - 0  # TODO
                              ws_account_rec.acct_last_activity.toordinal())

    if ws_days_inactive > 365:
        ws_account_rec.acct_status = 'D'
        mark_dormant_C10(ws_account_rec)

def mark_dormant_C10(ws_account_rec):
    """Marks an account as dormant."""
    ws_process_date: datetime.date = datetime.date(2024, 1, 1)
    ws_account_rec.acct_status_desc = 'DORMANT'
    ws_account_rec.acct_dormant_date = ws_process_date
    rewrite_account_record(ws_account_rec)
    send_dormant_notice_C10()

def rewrite_account_record(ws_account_rec):
    """Rewrites the account record in the account file."""
    pass

def send_dormant_notice_C10():
    """Sends a dormant account notice."""
    ws_notif_type: str = 'dormant_notice'
    ws_notif_channel: str = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject: str = 'Important: Your account is dormant'
    send_notification_C10(ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)

def send_notification_C10(ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str):
    """Sends a notification."""
    pass

def escheatment_processing_C10():
    """Processes escheatment for dormant accounts."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = read_account_file()
            if ws_account_rec.acct_status == 'D':
                check_escheatment_C10(ws_account_rec)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

@dataclass
class AccountRecC10:
    acct_status: str
    acct_last_activity: datetime.date
    acct_status_desc: str
    acct_dormant_date: datetime.date
    acct_balance: Decimal
    acct_id: str
    acct_owner_name: str
    acct_owner_address: str
    acct_pending_trans: int
    acct_loan_link: str
    acct_close_date: datetime.date

def check_escheatment_C10(ws_account_rec: AccountRecC10):
    """Checks if an account is eligible for escheatment."""
    ws_process_date: datetime.date = datetime.date(2024, 1, 1)
    ws_escheat_years: int = 5
    ws_dormant_years: float = ((ws_process_date.toordinal() - 0  # TODO
                                  ws_account_rec.acct_dormant_date.toordinal()) / 365)

    if ws_dormant_years >= ws_escheat_years:
        escheat_account_C10(ws_account_rec)

def escheat_account_C10(ws_account_rec: AccountRecC10):
    """Escheats an account."""
    ws_account_rec.acct_status = 'E'
    ws_escheat_amount: Decimal = ws_account_rec.acct_balance
    ws_account_rec.acct_balance = Decimal("0")
    create_escheat_record_C10(ws_account_rec, ws_escheat_amount)
    rewrite_account_record(ws_account_rec)

def create_escheat_record_C10(ws_account_rec: AccountRecC10, ws_escheat_amount: Decimal):
    """Creates an escheat record."""
    ws_process_date: datetime.date = datetime.date(2024, 1, 1)
    ws_escheat_record = EscheatRecordC10()
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_account = ws_account_rec.acct_id
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_amount = ws_escheat_amount
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_date = ws_process_date
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_owner = ws_account_rec.acct_owner_name
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_address = ws_account_rec.acct_owner_address
    write_escheat_record(ws_escheat_record)

@dataclass
class EscheatRecordC10:
    escheat_account: str
    escheat_amount: Decimal
    escheat_date: datetime.date
    escheat_owner: str
    escheat_address: str

def write_escheat_record(escheat_record):
    """Writes an escheat record to the escheat file."""
    pass

def account_closure_C10():
    """Processes account closures."""
    ws_close_request: str = 'Y'

    if ws_close_request == 'Y':
        validate_closure_C10()
        if ws_closure_valid == 'Y':
            process_closure_C10()
        else:
            reject_closure_C10()

def validate_closure_C10():
    """Validates an account closure request."""
    global ws_closure_valid, ws_closure_reject
    ws_account_rec: AccountRecC10 = AccountRecC10(
        acct_status='A',
        acct_last_activity=datetime.date(2023, 1, 1),
        acct_status_desc='',
        acct_dormant_date=datetime.date(2023, 1, 1),
        acct_balance=Decimal("100.00"),
        acct_id='12345',
        acct_owner_name='John Doe',
        acct_owner_address='123 Main St',
        acct_pending_trans=0,
        acct_loan_link='',
        acct_close_date=datetime.date(2023,1,1)
    )
    ws_closure_valid: str = 'Y'
    ws_closure_reject: str = ''

    if ws_account_rec.acct_balance < 0:
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'NEGATIVE BALANCE'

    if ws_account_rec.acct_pending_trans > 0:
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'PENDING TRANSACTIONS'

    if ws_account_rec.acct_loan_link != '':
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'LINKED LOAN EXISTS'

def process_closure_C10():
    """Processes an account closure."""
    global ws_closure_valid, ws_closure_reject
    ws_account_rec: AccountRecC10 = AccountRecC10(
        acct_status='A',
        acct_last_activity=datetime.date(2023, 1, 1),
        acct_status_desc='',
        acct_dormant_date=datetime.date(2023, 1, 1),
        acct_balance=Decimal("100.00"),
        acct_id='12345',
        acct_owner_name='John Doe',
        acct_owner_address='123 Main St',
        acct_pending_trans=0,
        acct_loan_link='',
        acct_close_date=datetime.date(2023,1,1)
    )
    ws_process_date: datetime.date = datetime.date(2024, 1, 1)
    ws_final_balance: Decimal = ws_account_rec.acct_balance
    disburse_balance_C10(ws_account_rec, ws_final_balance)
    ws_account_rec.acct_status = 'C'
    ws_account_rec.acct_close_date = ws_process_date
    rewrite_account_record(ws_account_rec)
    archive_account_C10(ws_account_rec, ws_process_date)

def disburse_balance_C10(ws_account_rec: AccountRecC10, ws_final_balance: Decimal):
    """Disburses the remaining balance of an account."""
    if ws_final_balance > 0:
        ws_check_record = CheckRecordC10()
        ws_check_record.check_from_account = ws_account_rec.acct_id
        ws_check_record.check_amount = ws_final_balance
        ws_check_record.check_memo = 'ACCOUNT CLOSURE'
        ws_check_record.check_payee = ws_account_rec.acct_owner_name
        write_check_record(ws_check_record)

@dataclass
class CheckRecordC10:
    check_from_account: str
    check_amount: Decimal
    check_memo: str
    check_payee:

    pass

def card_blocking_C11() -> None:
    """Blocks a card."""
    global CARD_STATUS
    global WS_BLOCK_REASON
    global CARD_BLOCK_REASON
    global WS_PROCESS_DATE
    global CARD_BLOCK_DATE
    global WS_CARD_RECORD
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE
    global WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL
    global WS_NOTIF_BODY

    CARD_STATUS = 'B'
    CARD_BLOCK_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_BLOCK_REASON
    CARD_BLOCK_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    #REWRITE CARD_RECORD FROM WS_CARD_RECORD
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'card_blocked'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS'
    WS_NOTIF_BODY = 'Your card has been blocked: ' + WS_BLOCK_REASON
    send_notification_C11()

def wire_transfer_C11() -> None:
    """Handles wire transfers."""
    validate_wire_request_C11()
    if WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
        ofac_screening_C11()
        if WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
            process_wire_C11()
            send_confirmation_C11()
        else:
            reject_wire_C11()

def validate_wire_request_C11() -> None:
    """Validates a wire transfer request."""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    global WS_WIRE_REJECT
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    global WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
    global WS_CTR_REQUIRED

    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == '':
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

def ofac_screening_C11() -> None:
    """Screens a wire transfer request against OFAC."""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR
    global WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    global OFAC_SEARCH_NAME
    global OFAC_REQUEST
    global OFAC_RESPONSE
    global OFAC_MATCH_FOUND
    global OFAC_MATCH_SCORE
    global WS_WIRE_REJECT
    global WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
    global OFAC_SEARCH_BANK

    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    #CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC_REQUEST OFAC_RESPONSE
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
    #CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC_REQUEST OFAC_RESPONSE
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

def process_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Processes a wire transfer."""
    debit_originator_C11()
    create_wire_message_C11()
    transmit_wire_C11()
    record_wire_C11()

def debit_originator_C11() -> None:
    """Debits the originator's account."""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    global WS_WIRE_FEE

    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def create_wire_message_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE
    global WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE
    global SWIFT_STATUS
    global WS_WIRE_STATUS

    #CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE
    if SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
    else:
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
        reverse_debit_C11()

    #INITIALIZE WS_WIRE_RECORD
    WIRE_REF  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    WIRE_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WIRE_STATUS  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_STATUS
    WIRE_FROM_ACCT = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
    WIRE_TO_ACCT = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
    WIRE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    #WRITE WIRE_RECORD FROM WS_WIRE_RECORD
    pass

def reverse_debit_C11() -> None:
    """Reverses the debit if the wire fails."""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    global WS_WIRE_FEE

    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def send_confirmation_C11() -> None:

def ach_processing_C11() -> None:

    #READ ACH_INPUT_FILE INTO WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER
    WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_FILE_ID

def validate_ach_entries_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID
    global ACH_ROUTING
    global WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    global ACH_ACCOUNT
    global ACH_AMOUNT
    global WS_VALID_ENTRIES
    global WS_INVALID_ENTRIES

    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'
    if not ACH_ROUTING.isnumeric():
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'
    if ACH_ACCOUNT == '':
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
    if ACH_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1
    else:
        WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1

def process_ach_credits_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY
    global WS_FOUND_FLAG
    global ACH_AMOUNT
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    global WS_CREDITS_POSTED
    global WS_TOTAL_CREDITS
    global WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE

    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        update_account_C11()
        WS_CREDITS_POSTED += 1
        WS_TOTAL_CREDITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def process_ach_debits_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY
    global WS_FOUND_FLAG
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    global ACH_AMOUNT
    global WS_DEBITS_POSTED
    global WS_TOTAL_DEBITS
    global WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE

    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE >= ACH_AMOUNT:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
            update_account_C11()
            WS_DEBITS_POSTED += 1
            WS_TOTAL_DEBITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        else:
            WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R01'
            create_return_entry_C11()
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def generate_ach_return_C11() -> None:
    """Generates ACH return file if needed."""
    global WS_RETURN_COUNT

    if WS_RETURN_COUNT > 0:
        create_return_file_C11()

def create_return_entry_C11() -> None:
    """Creates a single ACH return entry."""
    global WS_ACH_RETURN_ENTRY
    global ACH_TRACE_NUMBER
    global RETURN_ORIG_TRACE
    global WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    global RETURN_CODE
    global ACH_AMOUNT
    global RETURN_AMOUNT
    global ACH_ACCOUNT
    global RETURN_ACCOUNT
    global WS_RETURN_COUNT

    #INITIALIZE WS_ACH_RETURN_ENTRY
    RETURN_ORIG_TRACE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_TRACE_NUMBER
    RETURN_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    RETURN_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    RETURN_ACCOUNT  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    WS_RETURN_COUNT += 1
    #WRITE ACH_RETURN_RECORD FROM WS_ACH_RETURN_ENTRY
    pass

def create_return_file_C11() -> None:
    """Creates the ACH return file."""
    global ACH_RETURN_FILE

    #OPEN OUTPUT ACH_RETURN_FILE
    write_return_header_C11()
    write_return_entries_C11()
    write_return_trailer_C11()
    #CLOSE ACH_RETURN_FILE

def write_return_header_C11() -> None:
    """Writes the ACH return file header."""
    global WS_RETURN_HEADER
    global RETURN_RECORD_TYPE
    global RETURN_PRIORITY_CODE
    global WS_OUR_ROUTING
    global RETURN_IMMEDIATE_DEST
    global WS_OUR_COMPANY_ID
    global RETURN_IMMEDIATE_ORIGIN
    global RETURN_FILE_DATE

    #INITIALIZE WS_RETURN_HEADER
    RETURN_RECORD_TYPE = '1'
    RETURN_PRIORITY_CODE = '01'
    RETURN_IMMEDIATE_DEST  = None  # TODO: was WS_OUR_ROUTING
    RETURN_IMMEDIATE_ORIGIN  = None  # TODO: was WS_OUR_COMPANY_ID
    RETURN_FILE_DATE = None #FUNCTION CURRENT_DATE
    #WRITE ACH_RETURN_RECORD FROM WS_RETURN_HEADER
    pass

def write_return_entries_C11() -> None:
    """Writes the ACH return entries."""
    global WS_RETURN_IDX
    global WS_RETURN_COUNT
    global ACH_RETURN_RECORD

    WS_RETURN_IDX = 0 # Initialize WS_RETURN_IDX
    while WS_RETURN_IDX > WS_RETURN_COUNT:
        #WRITE ACH_RETURN_RECORD FROM WS_RETURN_ENTRY(WS_RETURN_IDX)
        WS_RETURN_IDX += 1

def write_return_trailer_C11() -> None:
    """Writes the ACH return file trailer."""
    global WS_RETURN_TRAILER
    global RETURN_RECORD_TYPE
    global WS_RETURN_COUNT
    global RETURN_ENTRY_COUNT
    global WS_RETURN_TOTAL
    global RETURN_TOTAL_AMOUNT

    #INITIALIZE WS_RETURN_TRAILER
    RETURN_RECORD_TYPE = '9'
    RETURN_ENTRY_COUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_RETURN_COUNT
    RETURN_TOTAL_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_RETURN_TOTAL
    #WRITE ACH_RETURN_RECORD FROM WS_RETURN_TRAILER
    pass

def statement_generation_C11() -> None:
    """Generates account statements."""
    prepare_statement_data_C11()
    generate_account_summary_C11()
    generate_transaction_detail_C11()
    calculate_statement_totals_C11()
    format_statement_C11()
    deliver_statement_C11()

import datetime

def move_ws_file_result_to_file_err_msg_C13(ws_file_result: str, file_err_msg: str) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    file_err_msg = ws_file_result
    pass

def move_function_current_date_to_file_err_timestamp_C13(file_err_timestamp: datetime.datetime) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    file_err_timestamp = datetime.datetime.now()
    pass

def write_file_error_record_from_ws_file_error_log_C13(file_error_record: str, ws_file_error_log: str) -> None:
    """Write file_error_record from ws_file_error_log."""
    file_error_record = ws_file_error_log
    pass

def logging_utilities_C13() -> None:
    """Calls logging utility functions."""
    log_info_C13()
    log_warning_C13()
    log_error_C13()
    pass

def log_info_C13() -> None:
    """Logs an informational message."""
    log_level: str = 'INFO'
    log_message: str = ws_log_message
    log_timestamp: datetime.datetime = datetime.datetime.now()
    log_record: str = ws_log_entry
    pass

def log_warning_C13() -> None:
    """Logs a warning message."""
    log_level: str = 'WARN'
    log_message: str = ws_log_message
    log_timestamp: datetime.datetime = datetime.datetime.now()
    log_record: str = ws_log_entry
    pass

def log_error_C13() -> None:
    """Logs an error message."""
    log_level: str = 'ERROR'
    log_message: str = ws_log_message
    log_timestamp: datetime.datetime = datetime.datetime.now()
    log_record: str = ws_log_entry
    pass

def error_handling_C13() -> None:
    """Handles errors."""
    format_error_C13()
    display_error_C13()
    write_error_log_C13()
    pass

def format_error_C13() -> None:
    """Formats the error message."""
    global ws_formatted_error
    ws_formatted_error = f"ERROR: {ws_error_code} - {ws_error_msg}"
    pass

def display_error_C13() -> None:
    """Displays the formatted error."""
    print(ws_formatted_error)
    pass

def write_error_log_C13() -> None:
    """Writes the error to the error log."""
    global ws_error_log_rec
    ws_error_log_rec = WsErrorLogRecC13()
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_code = ws_error_code
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_msg = ws_error_msg
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_timestamp = datetime.datetime.now()
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_program = ws_program_name
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_paragraph = ws_paragraph_name
    error_log_record: str = str(ws_error_log_rec)
    pass

ws_log_entry: str = ""
ws_program_name: str = ""
ws_paragraph_name: str = ""
ws_error_code: str = ""
ws_error_msg: str = ""
ws_formatted_error: str = ""

def treasury_management_C13() -> None:
    """Manages treasury functions."""
    calculate_cash_position_C13()
    project_cash_flows_C13()
    manage_reserves_C13()
    manage_investments_C13()
    manage_borrowings_C13()
    pass

def calculate_cash_position_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates the cash position."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position = Decimal("0.00")
    sum_vault_cash_C13()
    sum_fed_account_C13()
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13()
    pass

def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the vault cash."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            vault_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
            global ws_treasury_management_C13
            ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position += vault_balance
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    pass

def sum_fed_account_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the fed account balance."""
    ws_fed_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position += ws_fed_balance
    pass

def sum_correspondent_balances_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the correspondent balances."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            corr_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
            global ws_treasury_management_C13
            ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position += corr_balance
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    pass

def project_cash_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects the cash flows."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_inflows = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_outflows = Decimal("0.00")
    project_loan_payments_C13()
    project_deposit_flows_C13()
    project_investment_maturities_C13()
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_net_position = (
        ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position + ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_inflows - 0  # TODO
        ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_outflows)
    pass

def project_loan_payments_C13() -> None:
    """Projects loan payments."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_projection_date: datetime.datetime = datetime.datetime.now()
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            loan_pmt_date: datetime.datetime = datetime.datetime.now()
            loan_pmt_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

            if loan_pmt_date <= ws_projection_date:
                global ws_treasury_management_C13
                ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_inflows += loan_pmt_amount
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    pass

def project_deposit_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects deposit flows."""
    ws_avg_daily_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_projection_days: int = 30
    ws_avg_daily_withdrawals: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_expected_deposits: Decimal = ws_avg_daily_deposits * ws_projection_days
    ws_expected_withdrawals: Decimal = ws_avg_daily_withdrawals * ws_projection_days
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_inflows += ws_expected_deposits
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_outflows += ws_expected_withdrawals
    pass

def project_investment_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Projects investment maturities."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_projection_date: datetime.datetime = datetime.datetime.now()
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            inv_maturity_date: datetime.datetime = datetime.datetime.now()
            inv_par_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
            if inv_maturity_date <= ws_projection_date:
                global ws_treasury_management_C13
                ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_inflows += inv_par_value
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    pass

def manage_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Manages reserves."""
    calculate_reserve_requirement_C13()
    check_reserve_position_C13()
    ws_reserve_deficiency: str = 'N'
    if ws_reserve_deficiency == 'Y':
        cover_reserve_shortfall_C13()
    else:
        invest_excess_reserves_C13()
    pass

def calculate_reserve_requirement_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates the reserve requirement."""
    ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_reserve_ratio: Decimal = Decimal("0.10")
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_reserve_requirement = ws_total_deposits * ws_reserve_ratio
    pass

def check_reserve_position_C13() -> None:
    """Checks the reserve position."""
    ws_fed_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_excess_reserves = (
        ws_fed_balance - ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_reserve_requirement)
    ws_reserve_deficiency: str = 'N'
    if ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_excess_reserves < 0:
        ws_reserve_deficiency = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_reserve_deficiency = 'N'
    pass

def cover_reserve_shortfall_C13() -> None:
    """Covers a reserve shortfall."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_shortfall_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00") - ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_excess_reserves
    borrow_fed_funds_C13()
    pass

def borrow_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Borrows fed funds."""
    ff_trans_type: str = 'BORROW'
    ws_shortfall_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ff_amount: Decimal = ws_shortfall_amount
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ff_rate: Decimal = ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_fed_funds_rate
    ws_process_date: datetime.datetime = datetime.datetime.now()
    ff_settle_date: datetime.datetime = ws_process_date
    ff_maturity_date: int = ws_process_date.toordinal() + 1
    pass

def invest_excess_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Invests excess reserves."""
    ws_min_invest_amount: Decimal = Decimal("10000.00")
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    if ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_excess_reserves > ws_min_invest_amount:
        sell_fed_funds_C13()
    pass

def sell_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Sells fed funds."""
    ff_trans_type: str = 'SELL'
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ff_amount: Decimal = ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_excess_reserves
    ff_rate: Decimal = ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_fed_funds_rate
    ws_process_date: datetime.datetime = datetime.datetime.now()
    ff_settle_date: datetime.datetime = ws_process_date
    ff_maturity_date: int = ws_process_date.toordinal() + 1
    pass

def manage_investments_C13() -> None:
    """Manages investments."""
    review_investment_portfolio_C13()
    execute_investment_strategy_C13()
    mark_to_market_C13()
    pass

def review_investment_portfolio_C13() -> None:
    """Reviews the investment portfolio."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_investment_pool = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_avg_yield: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_avg_duration: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_yield: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_duration: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_inv_count: int = 0
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            inv_market_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
            inv_yield: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
            inv_duration: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
            ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_investment_pool += inv_market_value
            ws_total_yield += inv_yield
            ws_total_duration += inv_duration
            ws_inv_count += 1
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_inv_count > 0:
        ws_avg_yield = ws_total_yield / ws_inv_count
        ws_avg_duration = ws_total_duration / ws_inv_count
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    pass

def execute_investment_strategy_C13() -> None:
    """Executes the investment strategy."""
    ws_rate_outlook: str = 'STABLE'
    if ws_rate_outlook == 'RISING':
        shorten_duration_C13()
    elif ws_rate_outlook == 'FALLING':
        extend_duration_C13()
    elif ws_rate_outlook == 'STABLE':
        maintain_position_C13()
    pass

def shorten_duration_C13() -> None:
    """Shortens portfolio duration."""
    print('STRATEGY: SHORTENING PORTFOLIO DURATION')
    pass

def extend_duration_C13() -> None:
    """Extends portfolio duration."""
    print('STRATEGY: EXTENDING PORTFOLIO DURATION')
    pass

def maintain_position_C13() -> None:
    """Maintains current portfolio position."""
    print('STRATEGY: MAINTAINING CURRENT POSITION')
    pass

def mark_to_market_C13() -> None:
    """Marks investments to market."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            inv_cusip: str = ""
            inv_par_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
            inv_book_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
            inv_market_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
            ws_market_price: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
            inv_unrealized_gl: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

            get_market_price_C13(inv_cusip, ws_market_price)
            inv_market_value = inv_par_value * ws_market_price / Decimal("100.00")
            inv_unrealized_gl = inv_market_value - inv_book_value
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    pass

def get_market_price_C13(inv_cusip: str, ws_market_price: Decimal) -> None:
    """Gets the market price for a bond."""
    ws_cusip_lookup: str = inv_cusip
    ws_market_price = Decimal("100.00")
    pass

def manage_borrowings_C13() -> None:
    """Manages borrowings."""
    review_borrowing_capacity_C13()
    optimize_funding_mix_C13()
    manage_maturities_C13()
    pass

def review_borrowing_capacity_C13() -> None:
    """Reviews the borrowing capacity."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_borrowing_capacity = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_fhlb_capacity: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_repo_capacity: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_credit_line_avail: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_fhlb_capacity
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_repo_capacity
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_credit_line_avail
    pass

def optimize_funding_mix_C13() -> None:
    """Optimizes the funding mix."""
    ws_total_int_expense: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_wholesale_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_deposit_cost: Decimal = (
        ws_total_int_expense / ws_total_deposits * Decimal("100.00")) if ws_total_deposits else Decimal("0.00")
    if ws_deposit_cost > ws_wholesale_rate:
        print('CONSIDER WHOLESALE FUNDING')
    pass

def manage_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Manages borrowing maturities."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_process_date: datetime.datetime = datetime.datetime.now()
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            borrow_maturity: int = 0
            if borrow_maturity <= ws_process_date.toordinal() + 7:
                rollover_decision_C13()
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    pass

def rollover_decision_C13() -> None:
    """Decides whether to rollover or repay a borrowing."""
    borrow_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    if ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position >= borrow_amount:
        repay_borrowing_C13()
    else:
        rollover_borrowing_C13()
    pass

def repay_borrowing_C13() -> None:
    """Repays a borrowing."""
    borrow_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    borrow_status: str = ""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position -= borrow_amount
    borrow_status = 'REPAID'
    pass

def rollover_borrowing_C13() -> None:
    """Rolls over a borrowing."""
    ws_process_date: datetime.datetime = datetime.datetime.now()
    borrow_rollover_date: datetime.datetime = ws_process_date
    borrow_maturity: int = ws_process_date.toordinal() + 30
    ws_current_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    borrow_rate: Decimal = ws_current_rate
    pass

def liquidity_management_C13() -> None:
    """Manages liquidity."""
    calculate_liquidity_ratios_C13()
    monitor_liquidity_limits_C13()
    contingency_funding_plan_C13()
    pass

def capital_management_C14():
    """Capital management procedures."""
    calculate_capital_ratios_C14()
    risk_weighted_assets_C14()
    capital_planning_C14()
    stress_testing_C14()


def calculate_capital_ratios_C14():
    """Calculate capital ratios."""
    calculate_tier1_C14()
    calculate_tier2_C14()
    calculate_ratios_C14()


def calculate_tier1_C14():
    """Calculate Tier 1 capital."""
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_COMMON_STOCK_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 += WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_AOCI_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 -= None  # TODO: was WS_GOODWILL_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 -= None  # TODO: was WS_INTANGIBLES_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 -= WS_DTA_DEDUCTION_C14


def calculate_tier2_C14():
    """Calculate Tier 2 capital."""
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_SUB_DEBT_C14
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14 += WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE_C14
    WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14 = WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 + WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14


def calculate_ratios_C14():
    """Calculate capital ratios."""
    if WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 > 0:
        WS_CET1_RATIO_C14 = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14) * 100
        WS_CAPITAL_RATIO_C14 = (WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14 / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14) * 100
    if WS_TOTAL_ASSETS_C14 > 0:
        WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO_C14 = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 / WS_TOTAL_ASSETS_C14) * 100


def risk_weighted_assets_C14():
    """Calculate risk weighted assets."""
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    credit_rwa_C14()
    market_rwa_C14()
    operational_rwa_C14()


def credit_rwa_C14():
    """Calculate credit risk weighted assets."""
    WS_CASH_RWA_C14 = WS_CASH_POSITION_C14 * Decimal("0.00")
    WS_GOVT_RWA_C14 = WS_GOVT_SECURITIES_C14 * Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BANK_RWA_C14 = WS_BANK_DEPOSITS_C14 * Decimal("0.20")
    WS_MORTGAGE_RWA_C14 = WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES_C14 * Decimal("0.50")
    WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA_C14 = WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS_C14 * Decimal("1.00")
    WS_CONSUMER_RWA_C14 = WS_CONSUMER_LOANS_C14 * Decimal("1.00")
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_CASH_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_GOVT_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_BANK_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_MORTGAGE_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_CONSUMER_RWA_C14


def market_rwa_C14():
    """Calculate market risk weighted assets."""
    WS_MARKET_RWA_C14 = WS_TRADING_ASSETS_C14 * WS_MARKET_RISK_FACTOR_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_MARKET_RWA_C14


def operational_rwa_C14():
    """Calculate operational risk weighted assets."""
    WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA_C14 = WS_GROSS_INCOME_C14 * WS_OPERATIONAL_FACTOR_C14 * Decimal("12.5")
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA_C14


def capital_planning_C14():
    """Capital planning procedures."""
    project_capital_needs_C14()
    identify_capital_actions_C14()
    update_capital_plan_C14()


def project_capital_needs_C14():
    """Project capital needs."""
    WS_PROJECTED_RWA_C14 = WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 * (1 + WS_GROWTH_RATE_C14)
    WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL_C14 = WS_PROJECTED_RWA_C14 * WS_TARGET_RATIO_C14 / 100
    WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 = WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL_C14 - WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14


def identify_capital_actions_C14():
    """Identify capital actions."""
    if WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 > 0:
        if WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 <= WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS_PROJ_C14:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
        elif WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 <= WS_SUB_DEBT_CAPACITY_C14:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
        else:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'EQUITY RAISE'
    else:
        WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'


def update_capital_plan_C14():
    """Update capital plan."""
    WS_PLAN_UPDATE_DATE_C14 = datetime.date.today()
    PLAN_RECOMMENDED_ACTION_C14 = WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14
    PLAN_GAP_AMOUNT_C14  = None  # TODO: was WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14
    #rewrite capital_plan_record FROM ws_capital_plan
    pass

def compile_results_C14():
    """Compile stress test results."""
    print('STRESS TEST RESULTS COMPILED')
    if WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL_C14 == 'FAIL':
        remediation_actions_C14()


def calculate_stress_impact_C14():
    """Calculate stress impact."""
    WS_CREDIT_LOSSES_C14 = WS_LOAN_PORTFOLIO_C14 * WS_STRESS_LGD_C14 * WS_STRESS_PD_C14
    WS_MARKET_LOSSES_C14 = WS_TRADING_ASSETS_C14 * WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14 / 100
    WS_STRESS_LOSSES_C14 = WS_CREDIT_LOSSES_C14 + WS_MARKET_LOSSES_C14
    WS_STRESSED_CAPITAL_C14 = WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14 - WS_STRESS_LOSSES_C14
    WS_STRESSED_RATIO_C14 = (WS_STRESSED_CAPITAL_C14 / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14) * 100
    if WS_STRESSED_RATIO_C14 >= WS_MIN_CAPITAL_RATIO_C14:
        WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL_C14 = 'PASS'
    else:
        WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL_C14 = 'FAIL'

def post_journal_entry_C14():
    """Post journal entry."""
    validate_journal_entry_C14()
    if WS_JE_VALID_C14 == 'Y':
        post_to_accounts_C14()
        record_posting_C14()


def validate_journal_entry_C14():
    """Validate journal entry."""
    WS_JE_VALID_C14 = 'Y'
    WS_TOTAL_DEBITS_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_CREDITS_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_JE_IDX_C14 = 1
    while WS_JE_IDX_C14 <= 50:
        WS_TOTAL_DEBITS_C14 += JE_DEBIT_C14[WS_JE_IDX_C14-1]
        WS_TOTAL_CREDITS_C14 += JE_CREDIT_C14[WS_JE_IDX_C14-1]
        WS_JE_IDX_C14 += 1
    if WS_TOTAL_DEBITS_C14 != WS_TOTAL_CREDITS_C14:
        WS_JE_VALID_C14 = 'N'
        WS_JE_ERROR_C14 = 'OUT OF BALANCE'


def post_to_accounts_C14():
    """Post journal entry to GL accounts."""
    WS_JE_IDX_C14 = 1
    while WS_JE_IDX_C14 <= 50:
        if JE_GL_ACCOUNT_C14[WS_JE_IDX_C14-1] != "":
            WS_GL_ACCOUNT_C14 = JE_GL_ACCOUNT_C14[WS_JE_IDX_C14-1]
            #READ gl_master_file INTO ws_gl_record KEY IS gl_account
            WS_GL_DEBIT_BALANCE_C14 += JE_DEBIT_C14[WS_JE_IDX_C14-1]
            WS_GL_CREDIT_BALANCE_C14 += JE_CREDIT_C14[WS_JE_IDX_C14-1]
            WS_GL_NET_BALANCE_C14 = WS_GL_DEBIT_BALANCE_C14 - WS_GL_CREDIT_BALANCE_C14
            #REWRITE gl_record FROM ws_gl_record
        WS_JE_IDX_C14 += 1
    pass


def record_posting_C14():
    """Record the journal entry posting."""
    WS_JE_STATUS_C14 = 'POSTED'
    WS_JE_POST_DATE_C14 = datetime.date.today()
    #WRITE journal_record FROM ws_journal_entry
    pass

    WS_TOTAL_EQUITY_C14 = Decimal("0.00")

    while WS_EOF_FLAG_C14 == 'N':
        #READ gl_master_file INTO ws_gl_record
        #AT END
        #   MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        #NOT AT END
        if True:
            #Mock read
            WS_EOF_FLAG_C14 = 'Y'
            pass
        else:
            if GL_ASSET_C14:
                WS_TOTAL_ASSETS_C14 += WS_GL_NET_BALANCE_C14
            elif GL_LIABILITY_C14:
                WS_TOTAL_LIABILITIES_C14 += WS_GL_NET_BALANCE_C14
            elif GL_EQUITY_C14:
                WS_TOTAL_EQUITY_C14 += WS_GL_NET_BALANCE_C14
    WS_EOF_FLAG_C14 = 'N'
    WS_BALANCE_CHECK_

import datetime

def perform_if_C15(ws_gl_control_bal: Decimal, ws_subledger_total: Decimal, ws_recon_diff: Decimal) -> None:
    """Check reconciliation difference and log exception."""
    if ws_gl_control_bal - ws_subledger_total:
        if ws_recon_diff != Decimal('0'):
            log_recon_exception_C15()

def log_recon_exception_C15() -> None:
    """Initialize and write reconciliation exception record."""
    ws_recon_exception: dict = {}
    recon_exc_account: str = ws_gl_account
    recon_exc_diff: Decimal = ws_recon_diff
    recon_exc_date: datetime.date = datetime.date.today()
    recon_exception_record: dict = ws_recon_exception
    #WRITE recon_exception_record FROM ws_recon_exception
    pass

def intercompany_recon_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    load_ic_balances_C15()
    match_ic_pairs_C15()
    report_ic_differences_C15()

def load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """Load intercompany balances from file."""
    ws_ic_count: int = 0
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ intercompany_file INTO ws_ic_balance
        try:
            ws_ic_balance: dict = {} # read from file
            ws_ic_count += 1
            ws_ic_array[ws_ic_count] = ws_ic_balance
        except:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

ws_ic_array: List[dict] = [] # assuming dict for balance

def match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """Match intercompany pairs."""
    ws_ic_count: int = 0
    for ws_ic_idx in range(1, ws_ic_count + 1):
        find_ic_counterpart_C15(ws_ic_idx)

def find_ic_counterpart_C15(ws_ic_idx: int) -> None:
    """Find counterpart for given intercompany index."""
    ws_search_from: str = ic_from_entity[ws_ic_idx]
    ws_search_to: str = ic_to_entity[ws_ic_idx]
    ws_ic_count: int = 0
    for ws_ic_idx2 in range(1, ws_ic_count + 1):
        if ic_from_entity[ws_ic_idx2] == ws_search_to:
            if ic_to_entity[ws_ic_idx2] == ws_search_from:
                ws_ic_diff: Decimal = ic_amount[ws_ic_idx] + ic_amount[ws_ic_idx2]
                if ws_ic_diff != Decimal('0'):
                    log_ic_diff_C15(ws_search_from, ws_search_to, ws_ic_diff)
                break

ic_from_entity: dict = {}
ic_to_entity: dict = {}
ic_amount: dict = {}

def log_ic_diff_C15(ws_search_from: str, ws_search_to: str, ws_ic_diff: Decimal) -> None:
    """Log intercompany difference to file."""
    ws_ic_diff_rec: dict = {}
    icd_from: str = ws_search_from
    icd_to: str = ws_search_to
    icd_amount: Decimal = ws_ic_diff
    ic_diff_record: dict = ws_ic_diff_rec
    #WRITE ic_diff_record FROM ws_ic_diff_rec
    pass

    ws_audit_id: float = random_number() * 99999999999
    ws_audit_timestamp: datetime.date = datetime.date.today()
    ws_audit_user: str = ws_user_id
    ws_audit_action: str = ws_action_type
    ws_audit_session_id: str = ws_session_id

    pass

def random_number() -> float:
    """Return a random number between 0.0 and 1.0."""
    return random.random()

def archive_audit_logs_C15() -> None:
    """Archive audit logs at end of month."""
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        move_to_archive_C15()
        compress_archive_C15()

def move_to_archive_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ audit_file INTO ws_audit_record
        try:
            ws_audit_record: dict = {} # read from file
            if ws_audit_timestamp < ws_archive_date:
                archive_audit_record: dict = ws_audit_record
                #WRITE archive_audit_record FROM ws_audit_record
                #DELETE audit_file
                pass
        except:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def compress_archive_C15() -> None:
    """Compress audit archive."""
    print('COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE')

ws_end_of_month: str = ''
ws_audit_timestamp: datetime.date = datetime.date.today()
ws_archive_date: datetime.date = datetime.date.today()

def performance_monitoring_C15() -> None:

def collect_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collect performance metrics."""
    cpu_metrics_C15()
    memory_metrics_C15()
    io_metrics_C15()
    transaction_metrics_C15()

def cpu_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collect CPU metrics."""
    #CALL 'GETCPU' USING ws_cpu_utilization
    ws_cpu_utilization: int = 0
    if ws_cpu_utilization > 80:
        ws_cpu_alert: str = 'Y'

def memory_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collect memory metrics."""
    #CALL 'GETMEM' USING ws_memory_utilization
    ws_memory_utilization: int = 0
    if ws_memory_utilization > 85:
        ws_memory_alert: str = 'Y'

def io_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collect I/O metrics."""
    #CALL 'GETIO' USING ws_io_wait_time
    ws_io_wait_time: int = 0
    if ws_io_wait_time > ws_io_threshold:
        ws_io_alert: str = 'Y'

def transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collect transaction metrics."""
    ws_tps: Decimal = ws_trans_count / ws_elapsed_seconds
    ws_avg_response: Decimal = ws_total_response_time / ws_trans_count

ws_io_threshold: int = 0
ws_trans_count: int = 0
ws_elapsed_seconds: int = 0
ws_total_response_time: int = 0

def analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """Analyze performance metrics."""
    if ws_avg_response > ws_response_threshold:
        ws_perf_degraded: str = 'Y'
    if ws_tps < ws_min_tps_threshold:
        ws_throughput_low: str = 'Y'

def full_backup_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    if ws_day_of_week == 7:
        #CALL 'FULLBKUP' USING ws_backup_status
        ws_backup_status: str = '' # result of call
        if ws_backup_status == 'SUCCESS':
            ws_last_full_backup: datetime.date = datetime.date.today()

        ws_last_incr_backup: datetime.date = datetime.date.today()

        send_notification_C15()

def rotate_encryption_key_C15() -> None:
    """Rotate encryption key if old."""
    if ws_key_age_days > 90:
        #CALL 'GENKEY' USING ws_new_key
        ws_new_key: str = '' # result of call
        ws_old_key: str = ws_encryption_key
        ws_encryption_key: str = ws_new_key
        reencrypt_data_C15()

def reencrypt_data_C15() -> None:
    """Reencrypt data with new encryption key."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ encrypted_data_file INTO ws_enc_record
        try:
            ws_enc_record: dict = {}
            enc_data: str = ''
            #CALL 'AES256DEC' USING enc_data ws_old_key ws_decrypted_data
            ws_decrypted_data: str = ''
            #CALL 'AES256ENC' USING ws_decrypted_data ws_encryption_key ws_reencrypted_data
            ws_reenrypted_data: str = ''
            enc_data: str = ws_reenrypted_data
            encrypted_data_record: dict = ws_enc_record
            #REWRITE encrypted_data_record FROM ws_enc_record
            pass
        except:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def backup_keys_C15() -> None:
    """Backup encryption keys."""
    #CALL 'KEYBACKUP' USING ws_encryption_key ws_backup_status
    ws_backup_status: str = '' # result of call
    if ws_backup_status == 'SUCCESS':
        ws_last_key_backup: datetime.date = datetime.date.today()

        create_session_C15()

    """Create user session."""
    ws_session_id: float = random_number() * 999999999999
    ws_session_start: datetime.date = datetime.date.today()
    ws_session_expiry: int = datetime.date.today().toordinal() + 1

    if ws_failed_auth_count >= 3:

def detect_anomalies_C15() -> None:
    """Detect security anomalies."""
    if ws_login_count > ws_normal_login_threshold:
        ws_anomaly_detected: str = 'Y'
        ws_anomaly_type: str = 'EXCESSIVE LOGINS'
    if ws_trans_volume > ws_normal_trans_threshold:
        ws_anomaly_detected: str = 'Y'
        ws_anomaly_type: str = 'HIGH TRANSACTION VOLUME'

def scan_vulnerabilities_C15() -> None:
    """Scan system for vulnerabilities."""
    #CALL 'VULNSCAN' USING ws_scan_results
    ws_scan_results: str = '' # result of call
    ws_critical_vulns: int = 0
    if ws_critical_vulns > 0:
        alert_security_team_C15()

    send_notification_C15()

def customer_segmentation_C15() -> None:
    """Segment customers based on relationship value."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ customer_file INTO ws_cust_rec
        try:
            ws_cust_rec: dict = {}
            calculate_segment_C15(ws_cust_rec)
        except:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_segment_C15(ws_cust_rec: dict) -> None:
    """Calculate customer segment based on relationship value."""
    cust_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_loan_balances: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_investment_value: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_segment: str = ''
    ws_relationship_value: Decimal from decimal import Decimal
import datetime

cust_id: str = ''

def customer_segmentation_C15(ws_cust_rec: dict) -> None:
    """Segment customers based on relationship value."""
    cust_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_loan_balances: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_investment_value: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_relationship_value: Decimal = (cust_total_deposits + cust_loan_balances + cust_investment_value)
    if ws_relationship_value >= Decimal('1000000'):
        cust_segment = 'private_bank'
    elif ws_relationship_value >= Decimal('250000'):
        cust_segment = 'wealth_mgmt'
    elif ws_relationship_value >= Decimal('100000'):
        cust_segment = 'PREFERRED'
    elif ws_relationship_value >= Decimal('25000'):
        cust_segment = 'CORE'
    else:
        cust_segment = 'BASIC'
    customer_record: dict = ws_cust_rec
    #REWRITE customer_record FROM ws_cust_rec
    pass

def cross_sell_analysis_C15() -> None:
    """Analyze customers for cross-selling opportunities."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ customer_file INTO ws_cust_rec
        try:
            ws_cust_rec: dict = {}
            identify_opportunities_C15(ws_cust_rec)
        except:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def identify_opportunities_C15(ws_cust_rec: dict) -> None:
    """Identify cross-selling opportunities for a customer."""
    cust_has_checking: str = 'N'
    cust_has_savings: str = 'N'
    cust_has_mortgage: str = 'N'
    cust_income: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_has_investment: str = 'N'
    cust_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    if cust_has_checking == 'Y' and cust_has_savings == 'N':
        ws_opportunity: str = 'SAVINGS'
        create_lead_C15()
    if cust_has_mortgage == 'N' and cust_income > Decimal('75000'):
        ws_opportunity: str = 'MORTGAGE'
        create_lead_C15()
    if cust_has_investment == 'N' and cust_total_deposits > Decimal('50000'):
        ws_opportunity: str = 'INVESTMENT'
        create_lead_C15()

def create_lead_C15() -> None:
    """Create a lead for a cross-selling opportunity."""
    ws_lead_record: dict = {}
    lead_customer: str = cust_id
    lead_product: str = ws_opportunity
    lead_create_date: datetime.date = datetime.date.today()
    lead_status: str = 'NEW'
    lead_record: dict = ws_lead_record
    #WRITE lead_record FROM ws_lead_record
    pass



def retention_analysis_C15() -> None:
    """Analyze customers for retention risk."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ customer_file INTO ws_cust_rec
        try:
            ws_cust_rec: dict = {}
            calculate_churn_risk_C15(ws_cust_rec)
        except:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_churn_risk_C15(ws_cust_rec: dict) -> None:
    """Calculate churn risk score for a customer."""
    ws_churn_score: int = 0
    cust_balance_trend: str = ''
    cust_trans_frequency: str = ''
    cust_complaint_count: int = 0
    cust_tenure_months: int = 0
    if cust_balance_trend == 'DECLINING':
        ws_churn_score += 25
    if cust_trans_frequency == 'LOW':
        ws_churn_score += 20
    if cust_complaint_count > 2:
        ws_churn_score += 30
    if cust_tenure_months < 12:
        ws_churn_score += 15
    cust_churn_risk: int = ws_churn_score
    if ws_churn_score > 50:
        create_retention_alert()

def create_retention_alert():
    pass
